    BanditRLProof/ConditionalExpectationReward.lean

    1 import Mathlib.Probability.Independence.Conditional
2 import Mathlib.Probability.Kernel.CompProdEqIff
3 import BanditRLProof.RewardKernel
4 import BanditRLProof.RewardTraceLaw

7 /-!
8 # Conditional centered reward kernel bridge
9 
10 This module exposes a narrow `COND-EXPECT-REWARD` support leaf: if the
11 conditional-expectation kernel already identifies the next centered reward law
12 and its conditional integral is zero, then the ordinary conditional expectation
13 of that centered reward is zero.
14 
15 It also exposes a recursive trajectory-law identification from an initial
16 marginal and successor `condDistrib` laws.  It does not synthesize those process
17 laws from a concrete environment, prove arbitrary policy predictability, or
18 close final adaptive regret theorems.
19 -/
20 
21 namespace BanditRLProof
22 namespace ConditionalExpectationReward
23 
24 open MeasureTheory
25 
26 universe u v w x
27 
28 /--
29 Turn a trimmed-a.e. zero conditional-kernel integral into a true conditional
30 mean-zero statement.
31 
32 This is the generic kernel-facing bridge for `COND-EXPECT-REWARD`.  The hard
33 future work is to prove `h_kernel_zero` from a trajectory/kernel law; this
34 wrapper only connects that law-shaped hypothesis to Mathlib's `condExp`.
35 -/
36 theorem condExp_eq_zero_of_condExpKernel_integral_eq_zero
37     {Omega : Type u}
38     [mOmega : MeasurableSpace Omega] [StandardBorelSpace Omega]
39     (mu : Measure Omega) [IsFiniteMeasure mu]
40     (mcond : MeasurableSpace Omega) (hm : mcond <= mOmega)
41     (X : Omega -> Real)
42     (h_integrable : Integrable X mu)
43     (h_kernel_zero :
44       Filter.Eventually
45         (fun omega : Omega =>
46           integral
47             (ProbabilityTheory.condExpKernel
48               (Ω := Omega) (mΩ := mOmega) mu mcond omega)
49             X = 0)
50         (ae (mu.trim hm))) :
51     Filter.EventuallyEq (ae mu)
52       (@condExp Omega Real mcond mOmega _ _ _ mu X)
53       (fun _omega : Omega => (0 : Real)) := by
54   have h_kernel :
55       Filter.EventuallyEq (ae (mu.trim hm))
56         (@condExp Omega Real mcond mOmega _ _ _ mu X)
57         (fun omega : Omega =>
58           integral
59             (ProbabilityTheory.condExpKernel
60               (Ω := Omega) (mΩ := mOmega) mu mcond omega)
61             X) := by
62     exact
63       @ProbabilityTheory.condExp_ae_eq_trim_integral_condExpKernel
64         Omega Real mcond mOmega inferInstance mu inferInstance
65         inferInstance X inferInstance inferInstance hm h_integrable
66   have h_trim_zero :
67       Filter.EventuallyEq (ae (mu.trim hm))
68         (@condExp Omega Real mcond mOmega _ _ _ mu X)
69         (fun _omega : Omega => (0 : Real)) :=
70     h_kernel.trans h_kernel_zero
71   exact ae_eq_of_ae_eq_trim h_trim_zero
72 
73 /--
74 Monotonicity of the variance proxy in Mathlib's unconditional sub-Gaussian
75 MGF predicate.
76 
77 This small helper lets history-selected kernel witnesses with proxy `c` feed a
78 conditional theorem stated with a deterministic upper proxy `d`.
79 -/
80 theorem hasSubgaussianMGF_mono_varianceProxy
81     {Omega : Type u} [MeasurableSpace Omega]
82     {mu : Measure Omega} {X : Omega -> Real} {c d : NNReal}
83     (hcd : c <= d)
84     (h : ProbabilityTheory.HasSubgaussianMGF X c mu) :
85     ProbabilityTheory.HasSubgaussianMGF X d mu where
86   integrable_exp_mul := h.integrable_exp_mul
87   mgf_le := by
88     intro t
89     calc
90       ProbabilityTheory.mgf X mu t <=
91           Real.exp (((c : NNReal) : Real) * t ^ 2 / 2) :=
92         h.mgf_le t
93       _ <= Real.exp (((d : NNReal) : Real) * t ^ 2 / 2) := by
94         apply Real.exp_le_exp.mpr
95         have hcd_real : ((c : NNReal) : Real) <= ((d : NNReal) : Real) := by
96           exact_mod_cast hcd
97         have ht2 : 0 <= t ^ 2 := sq_nonneg t
98         nlinarith
99 
100 /--
101 A random variable measurable in the conditioning sigma-algebra is frozen by
102 the conditional-expectation kernel.
103 
104 The conclusion is a kernel equality on any countably generated target, rather
105 than a singleton reconstruction on a countable target.  The proof maps the
106 diagonal composition-product identity for `condExpKernel` through `X` and then
107 uses Mathlib's a.e. uniqueness theorem for finite kernels.
108 -/
109 theorem condExpKernel_map_eq_deterministic_of_measurable
110     {Omega : Type u} {Target : Type v}
111     [mOmega : MeasurableSpace Omega] [StandardBorelSpace Omega]
112     [mTarget : MeasurableSpace Target]
113     [MeasurableSpace.CountablyGenerated Target]
114     (mu : Measure Omega) [IsFiniteMeasure mu]
115     (mcond : MeasurableSpace Omega) (hm : mcond <= mOmega)
116     (X : Omega -> Target)
117     (hX : @Measurable Omega Target mcond mTarget X) :
118     Filter.EventuallyEq (ae (mu.trim hm))
119       (@ProbabilityTheory.Kernel.map Omega Omega mcond mOmega Target mTarget
120         (@ProbabilityTheory.condExpKernel Omega mOmega inferInstance mu inferInstance mcond) X)
121       (@ProbabilityTheory.Kernel.deterministic Omega Target mcond mTarget X hX) := by
122   have hdiag : @Measurable Omega (Omega × Omega) mOmega (mcond.prod mOmega)
123       (fun omega => (id omega, id omega)) :=
124     (measurable_id'' hm).prodMk measurable_id
125   have hprodMap : @Measurable (Omega × Omega) (Omega × Target)
126       (mcond.prod mOmega) (mcond.prod mTarget) (Prod.map id X) := by
127     simpa [Prod.map] using
128       (measurable_fst.prodMk ((hX.mono hm le_rfl).comp measurable_snd))
129   have hpair : @Measurable Omega (Omega × Target) mcond (mcond.prod mTarget)
130       (fun omega => (omega, X omega)) := measurable_id.prodMk hX
131   have hid : @Measurable Omega Omega mOmega mcond id := measurable_id'' hm
132   apply ProbabilityTheory.Kernel.ae_eq_of_compProd_eq
133   rw [Measure.compProd_map (hX.mono hm le_rfl),
134     @ProbabilityTheory.compProd_trim_condExpKernel Omega mcond mOmega
135       inferInstance mu inferInstance hm,
136     Measure.compProd_deterministic hX,
137     trim_eq_map hm]
138   rw [Measure.map_map hprodMap hdiag, Measure.map_map hpair hid]
139   rfl
140 
141 /--
142 Pointwise measure form of
143 `condExpKernel_map_eq_deterministic_of_measurable`.
144 -/
145 theorem condExpKernel_map_eq_dirac_of_measurable
146     {Omega : Type u} {Target : Type v}
147     [mOmega : MeasurableSpace Omega] [StandardBorelSpace Omega]
148     [mTarget : MeasurableSpace Target]
149     [MeasurableSpace.CountablyGenerated Target]
150     (mu : Measure Omega) [IsFiniteMeasure mu]
151     (mcond : MeasurableSpace Omega) (hm : mcond <= mOmega)
152     (X : Omega -> Target)
153     (hX : @Measurable Omega Target mcond mTarget X) :
154     Filter.Eventually
155       (fun omega =>
156         @Measure.map Omega Target mOmega mTarget X
157             (@ProbabilityTheory.condExpKernel Omega mOmega inferInstance mu inferInstance
158               mcond omega) =
159           @Measure.dirac Target mTarget (X omega))
160       (ae (mu.trim hm)) := by
161   filter_upwards [condExpKernel_map_eq_deterministic_of_measurable
162       (mOmega := mOmega) (mTarget := mTarget) mu mcond hm X hX] with omega homega
163   simpa [ProbabilityTheory.Kernel.map_apply _ (hX.mono hm le_rfl),
164     ProbabilityTheory.Kernel.deterministic_apply hX] using homega
165 
166 /--
167 Convert a `condDistrib` law into a `condExpKernel` pushforward law on a
168 countable target.
169 
170 Mathlib supplies eventwise equality between regular conditional distributions
171 and `condExpKernel` pushforwards.  This wrapper packages those singleton
172 equalities into a measure equality when the target type is countable.  It is a
173 local bridge from canonical `condDistrib` trajectory laws toward the
174 `condExpKernel` map-law consumers below; it does not itself construct the
175 trajectory law.
176 -/
177 theorem condExpKernel_map_eq_of_condDistrib_ae_eq_countable
178     {Omega : Type u} {Target : Type v} {Condition : Type w}
179     [mOmega : MeasurableSpace Omega] [StandardBorelSpace Omega]
180     [Nonempty Omega]
181     [mTarget : MeasurableSpace Target] [StandardBorelSpace Target]
182     [Nonempty Target] [MeasurableSingletonClass Target] [Countable Target]
183     [mCondition : MeasurableSpace Condition]
184     (mu : Measure Omega) [IsFiniteMeasure mu]
185     (X : Omega -> Target) (Y : Omega -> Condition)
186     (hX : @Measurable Omega Target mOmega mTarget X)
187     (hY : @Measurable Omega Condition mOmega mCondition Y)
188     (kernel : ProbabilityTheory.Kernel Condition Target)
189     (hcond :
190       Filter.EventuallyEq (ae (mu.map Y))
191         (ProbabilityTheory.condDistrib X Y mu)
192         kernel) :
193     Filter.Eventually
194       (fun omega : Omega =>
195         @Measure.map Omega Target mOmega mTarget X
196           (@ProbabilityTheory.condExpKernel Omega mOmega _ mu _
197             (mCondition.comap Y) omega) =
198         kernel (Y omega))
199       (ae mu) := by
200   have hcond_pullback :
201       Filter.Eventually
202         (fun omega : Omega =>
203           ProbabilityTheory.condDistrib X Y mu (Y omega) =
204           kernel (Y omega))
205         (ae mu) :=
206     MeasureTheory.ae_of_ae_map hY.aemeasurable hcond
207   have hsingle :
208       Filter.Eventually
209         (fun omega : Omega =>
210           forall z : Target,
211             ((@ProbabilityTheory.condExpKernel Omega mOmega _ mu _
212                   (mCondition.comap Y)).map X omega) {z} =
213               kernel (Y omega) {z})
214         (ae mu) := by
215     rw [ae_all_iff]
216     intro z
217     have h_event :
218         Filter.EventuallyEq (ae mu)
219           (fun omega : Omega =>
220             ProbabilityTheory.condDistrib X Y mu (Y omega) {z})
221           (fun omega : Omega =>
222             ((@ProbabilityTheory.condExpKernel Omega mOmega _ mu _
223                 (mCondition.comap Y)).map X omega) {z}) :=
224       ProbabilityTheory.condDistrib_apply_ae_eq_condExpKernel_map
225         (μ := mu) hX hY (MeasurableSet.singleton z)
226     filter_upwards [h_event, hcond_pullback] with omega h_event_eq hcond_eq
227     calc
228       ((@ProbabilityTheory.condExpKernel Omega mOmega _ mu _
229             (mCondition.comap Y)).map X omega) {z}
230           =
231         ProbabilityTheory.condDistrib X Y mu (Y omega) {z} := h_event_eq.symm
232       _ = kernel (Y omega) {z} := by rw [hcond_eq]
233   filter_upwards [hsingle] with omega hsingle_omega
234   have hkernel :
235       ((@ProbabilityTheory.condExpKernel Omega mOmega _ mu _
236           (mCondition.comap Y)).map X omega) =
237         kernel (Y omega) :=
238     Measure.ext_of_singleton hsingle_omega
239   simpa [ProbabilityTheory.Kernel.map_apply _ hX] using hkernel
240 
241 /--
242 Trim-a.e. form of the countable-target `condDistrib` to
243 `condExpKernel.map` bridge.
244 
245 The ordinary bridge gives equality almost everywhere for the ambient measure.
246 For each target singleton, both event-probability functions are measurable in
247 the conditioning sigma-algebra.  Mathlib's `ae_eq_trim_of_measurable` therefore
248 upgrades those scalar equalities to the trimmed measure, after which
249 countability reconstructs equality of the pushed-forward measures.
250 -/
251 theorem condExpKernel_map_eq_of_condDistrib_ae_eq_countable_trim
252     {Omega : Type u} {Target : Type v} {Condition : Type w}
253     [mOmega : MeasurableSpace Omega] [StandardBorelSpace Omega]
254     [Nonempty Omega]
255     [mTarget : MeasurableSpace Target] [StandardBorelSpace Target]
256     [Nonempty Target] [MeasurableSingletonClass Target] [Countable Target]
257     [mCondition : MeasurableSpace Condition]
258     (mu : Measure Omega) [IsFiniteMeasure mu]
259     (X : Omega -> Target) (Y : Omega -> Condition)
260     (hX : @Measurable Omega Target mOmega mTarget X)
261     (hY : @Measurable Omega Condition mOmega mCondition Y)
262     (kernel : ProbabilityTheory.Kernel Condition Target)
263     (hcond :
264       Filter.EventuallyEq (ae (mu.map Y))
265         (ProbabilityTheory.condDistrib X Y mu)
266         kernel) :
267     Filter.Eventually
268       (fun omega : Omega =>
269         @Measure.map Omega Target mOmega mTarget X
270           (@ProbabilityTheory.condExpKernel Omega mOmega _ mu _
271             (mCondition.comap Y) omega) =
272         kernel (Y omega))
273       (ae (mu.trim hY.comap_le)) := by
274   have hmap_ae :=
275     condExpKernel_map_eq_of_condDistrib_ae_eq_countable
276       mu X Y hX hY kernel hcond
277   have hsingle :
278       Filter.Eventually
279         (fun omega : Omega =>
280           forall z : Target,
281             (@Measure.map Omega Target mOmega mTarget X
282               (@ProbabilityTheory.condExpKernel Omega mOmega _ mu _
283                 (mCondition.comap Y) omega)) {z} =
284               kernel (Y omega) {z})
285         (ae (mu.trim hY.comap_le)) := by
286     rw [ae_all_iff]
287     intro z
288     have h_lhs_meas :
289         @Measurable Omega ENNReal
290           (mCondition.comap Y) inferInstance
291           (fun omega : Omega =>
292             (@Measure.map Omega Target mOmega mTarget X
293               (@ProbabilityTheory.condExpKernel Omega mOmega _ mu _
294                 (mCondition.comap Y) omega)) {z}) := by
295       have hkernel_meas :=
296         ((@ProbabilityTheory.condExpKernel Omega mOmega _ mu _
297             (mCondition.comap Y)).map X).measurable_coe
298           (MeasurableSet.singleton z)
299       simpa [ProbabilityTheory.Kernel.map_apply _ hX] using hkernel_meas
300     have h_rhs_meas :
301         @Measurable Omega ENNReal
302           (mCondition.comap Y) inferInstance
303           (fun omega : Omega => kernel (Y omega) {z}) := by
304       exact
305         (kernel.measurable_coe (MeasurableSet.singleton z)).comp
306           (Measurable.of_comap_le le_rfl)
307     have hsingle_ae :
308         Filter.EventuallyEq (ae mu)
309           (fun omega : Omega =>
310             (@Measure.map Omega Target mOmega mTarget X
311               (@ProbabilityTheory.condExpKernel Omega mOmega _ mu _
312                 (mCondition.comap Y) omega)) {z})
313           (fun omega : Omega => kernel (Y omega) {z}) := by
314       filter_upwards [hmap_ae] with omega hmap_eq
315       rw [hmap_eq]
316     exact
317       ae_eq_trim_of_measurable hY.comap_le h_lhs_meas h_rhs_meas hsingle_ae
318   filter_upwards [hsingle] with omega hsingle_omega
319   exact Measure.ext_of_singleton hsingle_omega
320 
321 /--
322 Convert a real-valued `condDistrib` law into a trimmed
323 `condExpKernel.map` law without assuming `Countable Real`.
324 
325 The proof first obtains equality on every rational left ray `Iic q`, upgrades
326 those scalar equalities to the trimmed measure using conditioning-space
327 measurability, and then reconstructs the full Borel measure by Mathlib's
328 countable rational-ray pi-system induction.
329 -/
330 theorem condExpKernel_map_eq_of_condDistrib_ae_eq_real_trim
331     {Omega : Type u} {Condition : Type v}
332     [mOmega : MeasurableSpace Omega] [StandardBorelSpace Omega]
333     [Nonempty Omega]
334     [mCondition : MeasurableSpace Condition]
335     (mu : Measure Omega) [IsFiniteMeasure mu]
336     (X : Omega -> Real) (Y : Omega -> Condition)
337     (hX : @Measurable Omega Real mOmega inferInstance X)
338     (hY : @Measurable Omega Condition mOmega mCondition Y)
339     (kernel : ProbabilityTheory.Kernel Condition Real)
340     [ProbabilityTheory.IsMarkovKernel kernel]
341     (hcond :
342       Filter.EventuallyEq (ae (mu.map Y))
343         (ProbabilityTheory.condDistrib X Y mu)
344         kernel) :
345     Filter.Eventually
346       (fun omega : Omega =>
347         @Measure.map Omega Real mOmega inferInstance X
348           (@ProbabilityTheory.condExpKernel Omega mOmega _ mu _
349             (mCondition.comap Y) omega) =
350         kernel (Y omega))
351       (ae (mu.trim hY.comap_le)) := by
352   let lhs : Omega -> Measure Real := fun omega =>
353     @Measure.map Omega Real mOmega inferInstance X
354       (@ProbabilityTheory.condExpKernel Omega mOmega _ mu _
355         (mCondition.comap Y) omega)
356   let rhs : Omega -> Measure Real := fun omega => kernel (Y omega)
357   have hcond_pullback :
358       Filter.Eventually
359         (fun omega : Omega =>
360           ProbabilityTheory.condDistrib X Y mu (Y omega) =
361             kernel (Y omega))
362         (ae mu) :=
363     MeasureTheory.ae_of_ae_map hY.aemeasurable hcond
364   have hIic :
365       forall q : Rat,
366         Filter.EventuallyEq (ae (mu.trim hY.comap_le))
367           (fun omega : Omega => lhs omega (Set.Iic (q : Real)))
368           (fun omega : Omega => rhs omega (Set.Iic (q : Real))) := by
369     intro q
370     have hevent :
371         Filter.EventuallyEq (ae mu)
372           (fun omega : Omega =>
373             ProbabilityTheory.condDistrib X Y mu (Y omega)
374               (Set.Iic (q : Real)))
375           (fun omega : Omega =>
376             lhs omega (Set.Iic (q : Real))) := by
377       simpa [lhs, ProbabilityTheory.Kernel.map_apply _ hX] using
378         (ProbabilityTheory.condDistrib_apply_ae_eq_condExpKernel_map
379           (μ := mu) hX hY (measurableSet_Iic :
380             MeasurableSet (Set.Iic (q : Real))))
381     have hambient :
382         Filter.EventuallyEq (ae mu)
383           (fun omega : Omega => lhs omega (Set.Iic (q : Real)))
384           (fun omega : Omega => rhs omega (Set.Iic (q : Real))) := by
385       filter_upwards [hevent, hcond_pullback] with omega heq hkernel
386       rw [← heq, hkernel]
387     have hlhs :
388         @Measurable Omega ENNReal
389           (mCondition.comap Y) inferInstance
390           (fun omega : Omega => lhs omega (Set.Iic (q : Real))) := by
391       have hmeas :=
392         ((@ProbabilityTheory.condExpKernel Omega mOmega _ mu _
393           (mCondition.comap Y)).map X).measurable_coe
394             (measurableSet_Iic :
395               MeasurableSet (Set.Iic (q : Real)))
396       simpa [lhs, ProbabilityTheory.Kernel.map_apply _ hX] using hmeas
397     have hrhs :
398         @Measurable Omega ENNReal
399           (mCondition.comap Y) inferInstance
400           (fun omega : Omega => rhs omega (Set.Iic (q : Real))) := by
401       exact
402         (kernel.measurable_coe
403           (measurableSet_Iic :
404             MeasurableSet (Set.Iic (q : Real)))).comp
405           (Measurable.of_comap_le le_rfl)
406     exact
407       ae_eq_trim_of_measurable hY.comap_le hlhs hrhs hambient
408   have huniv :
409       Filter.EventuallyEq (ae (mu.trim hY.comap_le))
410         (fun omega : Omega => lhs omega Set.univ)
411         (fun omega : Omega => rhs omega Set.univ) := by
412     have hevent :
413         Filter.EventuallyEq (ae mu)
414           (fun omega : Omega =>
415             ProbabilityTheory.condDistrib X Y mu (Y omega) Set.univ)
416           (fun omega : Omega => lhs omega Set.univ) := by
417       simpa [lhs, ProbabilityTheory.Kernel.map_apply _ hX] using
418         (ProbabilityTheory.condDistrib_apply_ae_eq_condExpKernel_map
419           (μ := mu) hX hY MeasurableSet.univ)
420     have hambient :
421         Filter.EventuallyEq (ae mu)
422           (fun omega : Omega => lhs omega Set.univ)
423           (fun omega : Omega => rhs omega Set.univ) := by
424       filter_upwards [hevent, hcond_pullback] with omega heq hkernel
425       rw [← heq, hkernel]
426     have hlhs :
427         @Measurable Omega ENNReal
428           (mCondition.comap Y) inferInstance
429           (fun omega : Omega => lhs omega Set.univ) := by
430       have hmeas :=
431         ((@ProbabilityTheory.condExpKernel Omega mOmega _ mu _
432           (mCondition.comap Y)).map X).measurable_coe MeasurableSet.univ
433       simpa [lhs, ProbabilityTheory.Kernel.map_apply _ hX] using hmeas
434     have hrhs :
435         @Measurable Omega ENNReal
436           (mCondition.comap Y) inferInstance
437           (fun omega : Omega => rhs omega Set.univ) := by
438       exact
439         (kernel.measurable_coe MeasurableSet.univ).comp
440           (Measurable.of_comap_le le_rfl)
441     exact
442       ae_eq_trim_of_measurable hY.comap_le hlhs hrhs hambient
443   have hsets :
444       Filter.Eventually
445         (fun omega : Omega =>
446           forall ⦃s : Set Real⦄, MeasurableSet s ->
447             lhs omega s = rhs omega s)
448         (ae (mu.trim hY.comap_le)) := by
449     apply @MeasurableSpace.ae_induction_on_inter
450       Real Omega (mCondition.comap Y) (mu.trim hY.comap_le)
451       (fun omega s => lhs omega s = rhs omega s)
452       (⋃ q : Rat, {Set.Iic (q : Real)}) inferInstance
453       Real.borel_eq_generateFrom_Iic_rat Real.isPiSystem_Iic_rat
454     · simp
455     · simp only [Set.iUnion_singleton_eq_range, Set.mem_range,
456         forall_exists_index, forall_apply_eq_imp_iff]
457       exact ae_all_iff.2 hIic
458     · filter_upwards [huniv] with omega huniv_eq s hs heq
459       rw [measure_compl hs (measure_ne_top _ _), heq, huniv_eq,
460         measure_compl hs (measure_ne_top _ _)]
461     · refine ae_of_all _ (fun omega f hdisj hf heq => ?_)
462       rw [measure_iUnion hdisj hf, measure_iUnion hdisj hf]
463       exact tsum_congr heq
464   filter_upwards [hsets] with omega homega
465   ext s hs
466   exact homega hs
467 
468 /--
469 If a measurable pushforward law is a Dirac measure, the original random
470 variable is a.e. constant.
471 
472 This is the small Mathlib bridge used to turn selected-action
473 `condExpKernel.map = dirac ...` laws into the conditional a.e. action equality
474 needed by the next-pair split-law route.
475 -/
476 theorem eventuallyEq_const_of_map_eq_dirac
477     {Omega : Type u} {Target : Type v}
478     [mOmega : MeasurableSpace Omega]
479     [mTarget : MeasurableSpace Target]
480     [MeasurableSingletonClass Target]
481     (mu : Measure Omega) (X : Omega -> Target) (x : Target)
482     (hX : @Measurable Omega Target mOmega mTarget X)
483     (hmap : @Measure.map Omega Target mOmega mTarget X mu = Measure.dirac x) :
484     Filter.EventuallyEq (ae mu) X (fun _omega : Omega => x) := by
485   have hdirac :
486       Filter.EventuallyEq (ae (Measure.dirac x))
487         (fun y : Target => y) (fun _y : Target => x) := by
488     simp [Filter.EventuallyEq]
489   have hmap_ae :
490       Filter.EventuallyEq (ae (@Measure.map Omega Target mOmega mTarget X mu))
491         (fun y : Target => y) (fun _y : Target => x) := by
492     simpa [hmap] using hdirac
493   have hpull :
494       Filter.Eventually
495         (fun omega : Omega => X omega = x)
496         (ae mu) := by
497     simpa [Filter.EventuallyEq] using
498       (MeasureTheory.ae_of_ae_map hX.aemeasurable hmap_ae)
499   simpa [Filter.EventuallyEq] using hpull
500 
501 /--
502 Build a pair pushforward law from a deterministic action side and a reward
503 pushforward law.
504 
505 This is the measure-level split helper behind the next-pair route, stripped of
506 filtration-specific hypotheses.  It is useful both for canonical trajectory
507 laws and for later ambient laws once the relevant `condExpKernel` has been
508 identified.
509 -/
510 theorem pair_map_eq_map_prod_mk_of_action_ae_eq_const_reward_map_eq
511     {Omega : Type u} {Action : Type v} {Reward : Type w}
512     [mOmega : MeasurableSpace Omega]
513     [MeasurableSpace Action] [MeasurableSpace Reward]
514     (mu : Measure Omega)
515     (nextAction : Omega -> Action) (nextReward : Omega -> Reward)
516     (selectedAction : Action) (selectedReward : Measure Reward)
517     (h_nextReward : @Measurable Omega Reward mOmega inferInstance nextReward)
518     (h_action_ae :
519       Filter.EventuallyEq (ae mu) nextAction
520         (fun _omega : Omega => selectedAction))
521     (h_reward_map_eq :
522       @Measure.map Omega Reward mOmega inferInstance nextReward mu =
523         selectedReward) :
524     @Measure.map Omega (Prod Action Reward) mOmega inferInstance
525         (fun omega : Omega => (nextAction omega, nextReward omega)) mu =
526       Measure.map (Prod.mk selectedAction) selectedReward := by
527   have h_pair_ae :
528       Filter.EventuallyEq (ae mu)
529         (fun omega : Omega => (nextAction omega, nextReward omega))
530         (fun omega : Omega => (selectedAction, nextReward omega)) := by
531     filter_upwards [h_action_ae] with omega h_action
532     simp [h_action]
533   have h_prod_mk_meas :
534       Measurable (Prod.mk selectedAction : Reward -> Prod Action Reward) := by
535     fun_prop
536   have h_map_map :
537       Measure.map (Prod.mk selectedAction)
538           (@Measure.map Omega Reward mOmega inferInstance nextReward mu) =
539         @Measure.map Omega (Prod Action Reward) mOmega inferInstance
540           (fun omega : Omega => (selectedAction, nextReward omega)) mu := by
541     rw [Measure.map_map h_prod_mk_meas h_nextReward]
542     rfl
543   calc
544     @Measure.map Omega (Prod Action Reward) mOmega inferInstance
545         (fun omega : Omega => (nextAction omega, nextReward omega)) mu =
546       @Measure.map Omega (Prod Action Reward) mOmega inferInstance
547         (fun omega : Omega => (selectedAction, nextReward omega)) mu := by
548         exact Measure.map_congr h_pair_ae
549     _ =
550       Measure.map (Prod.mk selectedAction)
551         (@Measure.map Omega Reward mOmega inferInstance nextReward mu) := by
552         exact h_map_map.symm
553     _ = Measure.map (Prod.mk selectedAction) selectedReward := by
554         rw [h_reward_map_eq]
555 
556 /--
557 Build a conditional next-pair product law from split action and reward laws.
558 
559 For each conditioning point, the action side freezes a.e. under the
560 `condExpKernel`, while the reward side supplies the selected reward measure.
561 This is the ambient `condExpKernel` form of
562 `pair_map_eq_map_prod_mk_of_action_ae_eq_const_reward_map_eq`.
563 -/
564 theorem condExpKernel_pair_map_eq_map_prod_mk_of_action_ae_reward_map_eq
565     {Omega : Type u} {Action : Type v} {Reward : Type w}
566     [mOmega : MeasurableSpace Omega] [StandardBorelSpace Omega]
567     [MeasurableSpace Action] [MeasurableSpace Reward]
568     (mu : Measure Omega) [IsFiniteMeasure mu]
569     (F : Filtration Nat mOmega) (i : Nat)
570     (nextAction : Omega -> Action) (nextReward : Omega -> Reward)
571     (selectedAction : Omega -> Action)
572     (selectedReward : Omega -> Measure Reward)
573     (h_nextReward :
574       @Measurable Omega Reward mOmega inferInstance nextReward)
575     (h_action_ae :
576       Filter.Eventually
577         (fun omega : Omega =>
578           Filter.EventuallyEq
579             (ae (@ProbabilityTheory.condExpKernel Omega mOmega _ mu _ (F i)
580               omega))
581             nextAction
582             (fun _y : Omega => selectedAction omega))
583         (ae (mu.trim (F.le i))))
584     (h_reward_map_eq :
585       Filter.Eventually
586         (fun omega : Omega =>
587           @Measure.map Omega Reward mOmega inferInstance nextReward
588             (@ProbabilityTheory.condExpKernel Omega mOmega _ mu _ (F i)
589               omega) =
590           selectedReward omega)
591         (ae (mu.trim (F.le i)))) :
592     Filter.Eventually
593       (fun omega : Omega =>
594         @Measure.map Omega (Prod Action Reward) mOmega inferInstance
595           (fun y : Omega => (nextAction y, nextReward y))
596           (@ProbabilityTheory.condExpKernel Omega mOmega _ mu _ (F i)
597             omega) =
598         Measure.map (Prod.mk (selectedAction omega))
599           (selectedReward omega))
600       (ae (mu.trim (F.le i))) := by
601   filter_upwards [h_action_ae, h_reward_map_eq] with omega h_action h_reward
602   exact
603     pair_map_eq_map_prod_mk_of_action_ae_eq_const_reward_map_eq
604       (mu := @ProbabilityTheory.condExpKernel Omega mOmega _ mu _ (F i)
605         omega)
606       (nextAction := nextAction)
607       (nextReward := nextReward)
608       (selectedAction := selectedAction omega)
609       (selectedReward := selectedReward omega)
610       h_nextReward h_action h_reward
611 
612 /--
613 Canonical reward-only `trajMeasure` selected-reward law in
614 `condExpKernel.map` form.
615 
616 This applies Mathlib's `Kernel.condDistrib_trajMeasure` theorem directly to
617 `RewardKernel.historyStepKernelFamily`, then uses the local countable-target
618 `condDistrib`-to-`condExpKernel.map` bridge.  Unlike the action/reward pair
619 trajectory route below, the canonical process here has only reward
620 coordinates, so its finite prefix is already the reward history consumed by
621 the policy, context, and state maps.
622 -/
623 theorem historyStepKernelFamily_selectedMeasure_condExpKernel_map_trajMeasure
624     {Context : Type x} {State : Type u} {Action : Type v}
625     {Reward : Type w}
626     [MeasurableSpace Context] [MeasurableSpace State]
627     [MeasurableSpace Action] [MeasurableSpace Reward]
628     [StandardBorelSpace Reward]
629     [StandardBorelSpace ((t : Nat) -> Reward)]
630     [Nonempty Reward] [Nonempty ((t : Nat) -> Reward)]
631     [MeasurableSingletonClass Reward] [Countable Reward]
632     (mu0 : Measure Reward)
633     [MeasureTheory.IsProbabilityMeasure mu0]
634     (rewardKernel : RewardKernel.MarkovRewardKernel (Prod Context Action) Reward)
635     (policy : Nat -> Policy.MeasurablePolicy State Action)
636     (context :
637       (n : Nat) -> ((i : Finset.Iic n) -> Reward) -> Context)
638     (state :
639       (n : Nat) -> ((i : Finset.Iic n) -> Reward) -> State)
640     (hcontext : forall n : Nat, Measurable (context n))
641     (hstate : forall n : Nat, Measurable (state n))
642     (n : Nat) :
643     let stepKernel :=
644       RewardKernel.historyStepKernelFamily rewardKernel policy context state
645         hcontext hstate
646     let trajMeasure :=
647       ProbabilityTheory.Kernel.trajMeasure
648         (X := fun _ : Nat => Reward) mu0 stepKernel
649     Filter.Eventually
650       (fun trajectory : (t : Nat) -> Reward =>
651         @Measure.map ((t : Nat) -> Reward) Reward
652           inferInstance inferInstance
653           (fun y : (t : Nat) -> Reward => y (n + 1))
654           (@ProbabilityTheory.condExpKernel
655             ((t : Nat) -> Reward) inferInstance _
656             trajMeasure _
657             ((inferInstance :
658               MeasurableSpace ((i : Finset.Iic n) -> Reward)).comap
659               (Preorder.frestrictLe n))
660             trajectory) =
661         RewardKernel.selectedMeasure rewardKernel
662           (context n (Preorder.frestrictLe n trajectory))
663           ((policy n).action
664             (state n (Preorder.frestrictLe n trajectory))))
665       (ae trajMeasure) := by
666   let stepKernel :=
667     RewardKernel.historyStepKernelFamily rewardKernel policy context state
668       hcontext hstate
669   let trajMeasure :=
670     ProbabilityTheory.Kernel.trajMeasure
671       (X := fun _ : Nat => Reward) mu0 stepKernel
672   let prefixMap :
673       ((t : Nat) -> Reward) -> ((i : Finset.Iic n) -> Reward) :=
674     Preorder.frestrictLe n
675   let nextReward : ((t : Nat) -> Reward) -> Reward :=
676     fun trajectory => trajectory (n + 1)
677   have h_nextReward : Measurable nextReward := by
678     exact measurable_pi_apply (n + 1)
679   have h_prefix : Measurable prefixMap := by
680     fun_prop
681   have hcond :
682       Filter.EventuallyEq (ae (trajMeasure.map prefixMap))
683         (ProbabilityTheory.condDistrib nextReward prefixMap trajMeasure)
684         (stepKernel n) := by
685     simpa [stepKernel, trajMeasure, prefixMap, nextReward] using
686       (ProbabilityTheory.Kernel.condDistrib_trajMeasure
687         (X := fun _ : Nat => Reward)
688         (a := n))
689   have hbridge :=
690     condExpKernel_map_eq_of_condDistrib_ae_eq_countable
691       (mu := trajMeasure)
692       (X := nextReward)
693       (Y := prefixMap)
694       h_nextReward h_prefix (stepKernel n) hcond
695   simpa [stepKernel, trajMeasure, prefixMap, nextReward,
696     RewardKernel.historyStepKernelFamily_apply] using hbridge
697 
698 /--
699 Trim-a.e. canonical reward-only `trajMeasure` selected-reward law.
700 
701 This is the source-facing strengthening of
702 `historyStepKernelFamily_selectedMeasure_condExpKernel_map_trajMeasure`.  It
703 uses the trim-aware countable-target bridge, so the conclusion is stated on
704 the finite reward-prefix conditioning sigma-algebra itself.
705 -/
706 theorem historyStepKernelFamily_selectedMeasure_condExpKernel_map_trajMeasure_trim
707     {Context : Type x} {State : Type u} {Action : Type v}
708     {Reward : Type w}
709     [MeasurableSpace Context] [MeasurableSpace State]
710     [MeasurableSpace Action] [MeasurableSpace Reward]
711     [StandardBorelSpace Reward]
712     [StandardBorelSpace ((t : Nat) -> Reward)]
713     [Nonempty Reward] [Nonempty ((t : Nat) -> Reward)]
714     [MeasurableSingletonClass Reward] [Countable Reward]
715     (mu0 : Measure Reward)
716     [MeasureTheory.IsProbabilityMeasure mu0]
717     (rewardKernel : RewardKernel.MarkovRewardKernel (Prod Context Action) Reward)
718     (policy : Nat -> Policy.MeasurablePolicy State Action)
719     (context :
720       (n : Nat) -> ((i : Finset.Iic n) -> Reward) -> Context)
721     (state :
722       (n : Nat) -> ((i : Finset.Iic n) -> Reward) -> State)
723     (hcontext : forall n : Nat, Measurable (context n))
724     (hstate : forall n : Nat, Measurable (state n))
725     (n : Nat) :
726     let stepKernel :=
727       RewardKernel.historyStepKernelFamily rewardKernel policy context state
728         hcontext hstate
729     let trajMeasure :=
730       ProbabilityTheory.Kernel.trajMeasure
731         (X := fun _ : Nat => Reward) mu0 stepKernel
732     Filter.Eventually
733       (fun trajectory : (t : Nat) -> Reward =>
734         @Measure.map ((t : Nat) -> Reward) Reward
735           inferInstance inferInstance
736           (fun y : (t : Nat) -> Reward => y (n + 1))
737           (@ProbabilityTheory.condExpKernel
738             ((t : Nat) -> Reward) inferInstance _
739             trajMeasure _
740             ((inferInstance :
741               MeasurableSpace ((i : Finset.Iic n) -> Reward)).comap
742               (Preorder.frestrictLe n))
743             trajectory) =
744         RewardKernel.selectedMeasure rewardKernel
745           (context n (Preorder.frestrictLe n trajectory))
746           ((policy n).action
747             (state n (Preorder.frestrictLe n trajectory))))
748       (ae
749         (trajMeasure.trim
750           (show
751             ((inferInstance :
752               MeasurableSpace ((i : Finset.Iic n) -> Reward)).comap
753                 (Preorder.frestrictLe n)) <=
754               (inferInstance :
755                 MeasurableSpace ((t : Nat) -> Reward)) from by
756             have hprefix :
757                 Measurable
758                   (Preorder.frestrictLe n :
759                     ((t : Nat) -> Reward) ->
760                       ((i : Finset.Iic n) -> Reward)) := by
761               fun_prop
762             exact hprefix.comap_le))) := by
763   let stepKernel :=
764     RewardKernel.historyStepKernelFamily rewardKernel policy context state
765       hcontext hstate
766   let trajMeasure :=
767     ProbabilityTheory.Kernel.trajMeasure
768       (X := fun _ : Nat => Reward) mu0 stepKernel
769   let prefixMap :
770       ((t : Nat) -> Reward) -> ((i : Finset.Iic n) -> Reward) :=
771     Preorder.frestrictLe n
772   let nextReward : ((t : Nat) -> Reward) -> Reward :=
773     fun trajectory => trajectory (n + 1)
774   have h_nextReward : Measurable nextReward := by
775     exact measurable_pi_apply (n + 1)
776   have h_prefix : Measurable prefixMap := by
777     fun_prop
778   have hcond :
779       Filter.EventuallyEq (ae (trajMeasure.map prefixMap))
780         (ProbabilityTheory.condDistrib nextReward prefixMap trajMeasure)
781         (stepKernel n) := by
782     simpa [stepKernel, trajMeasure, prefixMap, nextReward] using
783       (ProbabilityTheory.Kernel.condDistrib_trajMeasure
784         (X := fun _ : Nat => Reward)
785         (a := n))
786   have hbridge :=
787     condExpKernel_map_eq_of_condDistrib_ae_eq_countable_trim
788       (mu := trajMeasure)
789       (X := nextReward)
790       (Y := prefixMap)
791       h_nextReward h_prefix (stepKernel n) hcond
792   simpa [stepKernel, trajMeasure, prefixMap, nextReward,
793     RewardKernel.historyStepKernelFamily_apply] using hbridge
794 
795 /--
796 Identify an ambient reward trace with the canonical reward-only trajectory from
797 its initial marginal and successor conditional distributions.
798 
799 The complete-law step is supplied by the foundation-level reward-trace
800 uniqueness theorem.  This wrapper specializes its kernel family to
801 `RewardKernel.historyStepKernelFamily`, exposing the recursive process contract
802 needed by the ambient selected-reward transport below.
803 -/
804 theorem historyStepKernelFamily_identDistrib_trajMeasure_of_condDistrib
805     {Omega : Type u} {Context : Type v} {State : Type w}
806     {Action : Type x} {Reward : Type*}
807     [mOmega : MeasurableSpace Omega]
808     [MeasurableSpace Context] [MeasurableSpace State]
809     [MeasurableSpace Action] [MeasurableSpace Reward]
810     [StandardBorelSpace Reward] [Nonempty Reward]
811     (mu : Measure Omega) [IsFiniteMeasure mu]
812     (mu0 : Measure Reward) [IsProbabilityMeasure mu0]
813     (rewardKernel : RewardKernel.MarkovRewardKernel (Prod Context Action) Reward)
814     (policy : Nat -> Policy.MeasurablePolicy State Action)
815     (context :
816       (n : Nat) -> ((i : Finset.Iic n) -> Reward) -> Context)
817     (state :
818       (n : Nat) -> ((i : Finset.Iic n) -> Reward) -> State)
819     (hcontext : forall n : Nat, Measurable (context n))
820     (hstate : forall n : Nat, Measurable (state n))
821     (reward : Omega -> ((t : Nat) -> Reward))
822     (hreward : forall t : Nat,
823       Measurable (fun omega : Omega => reward omega t))
824     (hzero : Measure.map (fun omega : Omega => reward omega 0) mu = mu0)
825     (hcond : forall i : Nat,
826       ProbabilityTheory.condDistrib
827           (fun omega : Omega => reward omega (i + 1))
828           (fun omega : Omega => Preorder.frestrictLe i (reward omega))
829           mu =ᵐ[mu.map (fun omega : Omega =>
830             Preorder.frestrictLe i (reward omega))]
831         (RewardKernel.historyStepKernelFamily rewardKernel policy context state
832           hcontext hstate i)) :
833     ProbabilityTheory.IdentDistrib reward id mu
834       (ProbabilityTheory.Kernel.trajMeasure
835         (X := fun _ : Nat => Reward) mu0
836         (RewardKernel.historyStepKernelFamily rewardKernel policy context state
837           hcontext hstate)) := by
838   let stepKernel :=
839     RewardKernel.historyStepKernelFamily rewardKernel policy context state
840       hcontext hstate
841   have hmap :
842       Measure.map reward mu =
843         ProbabilityTheory.Kernel.trajMeasure
844           (X := fun _ : Nat => Reward) mu0 stepKernel := by
845     apply RewardKernel.rewardTrace_map_eq_trajMeasure_of_condDistrib
846       mu mu0 reward hreward stepKernel hzero
847     intro i
848     simpa [stepKernel, History.finiteRewardHistoryOfTrace] using hcond i
849   refine
850     ⟨(measurable_pi_lambda _ hreward).aemeasurable,
851       measurable_id.aemeasurable, ?_⟩
852   simpa [stepKernel] using hmap
853 
854 /--
855 Transport the canonical reward-only selected-reward law to an ambient reward
856 trace with the same complete distribution.
857 
858 The `IdentDistrib` contract is strictly upstream of the conditional law used
859 by the source layer.  Composing it with the finite-prefix/next-coordinate map
860 transports the relevant joint law from Mathlib's canonical `trajMeasure`.
861 The canonical `condDistrib_trajMeasure` factorization and disintegration
862 uniqueness then recover the ambient conditional distribution, after which the
863 countable-target trim bridge yields the requested `condExpKernel.map` law.
864 -/
865 theorem historyStepKernelFamily_selectedMeasure_condExpKernel_map_of_identDistrib_trajMeasure_trim
866     {Omega : Type u} {Context : Type v} {State : Type w}
867     {Action : Type x} {Reward : Type*}
868     [mOmega : MeasurableSpace Omega] [StandardBorelSpace Omega]
869     [MeasurableSpace Context] [MeasurableSpace State]
870     [MeasurableSpace Action] [MeasurableSpace Reward]
871     [StandardBorelSpace Reward]
872     [StandardBorelSpace ((t : Nat) -> Reward)]
873     [Nonempty Omega] [Nonempty Reward]
874     [Nonempty ((t : Nat) -> Reward)]
875     [MeasurableSingletonClass Reward] [Countable Reward]
876     (mu : Measure Omega) [IsFiniteMeasure mu]
877     (mu0 : Measure Reward) [IsProbabilityMeasure mu0]
878     (rewardKernel : RewardKernel.MarkovRewardKernel (Prod Context Action) Reward)
879     (policy : Nat -> Policy.MeasurablePolicy State Action)
880     (context :
881       (n : Nat) -> ((i : Finset.Iic n) -> Reward) -> Context)
882     (state :
883       (n : Nat) -> ((i : Finset.Iic n) -> Reward) -> State)
884     (hcontext : forall n : Nat, Measurable (context n))
885     (hstate : forall n : Nat, Measurable (state n))
886     (reward : Omega -> ((t : Nat) -> Reward))
887     (hreward : forall t : Nat,
888       Measurable (fun omega : Omega => reward omega t))
889     (hident :
890       ProbabilityTheory.IdentDistrib reward id mu
891         (ProbabilityTheory.Kernel.trajMeasure
892           (X := fun _ : Nat => Reward) mu0
893           (RewardKernel.historyStepKernelFamily rewardKernel policy context
894             state hcontext hstate)))
895     (n : Nat) :
896     Filter.Eventually
897       (fun omega : Omega =>
898         @Measure.map Omega Reward mOmega inferInstance
899           (fun y : Omega => reward y (n + 1))
900           (@ProbabilityTheory.condExpKernel Omega mOmega _ mu _
901             ((inferInstance :
902               MeasurableSpace ((i : Finset.Iic n) -> Reward)).comap
903               (fun y : Omega => Preorder.frestrictLe n (reward y)))
904             omega) =
905         RewardKernel.selectedMeasure rewardKernel
906           (context n (Preorder.frestrictLe n (reward omega)))
907           ((policy n).action
908             (state n (Preorder.frestrictLe n (reward omega)))))
909       (ae
910         (mu.trim
911           (show
912             ((inferInstance :
913               MeasurableSpace ((i : Finset.Iic n) -> Reward)).comap
914               (fun y : Omega => Preorder.frestrictLe n (reward y))) <=
915                 mOmega from by
916             exact
917               (History.measurable_finiteRewardHistoryOfTrace reward hreward n).comap_le))) := by
918   let stepKernel :=
919     RewardKernel.historyStepKernelFamily rewardKernel policy context state
920       hcontext hstate
921   let trajMeasure :=
922     ProbabilityTheory.Kernel.trajMeasure
923       (X := fun _ : Nat => Reward) mu0 stepKernel
924   let prefixAmbient : Omega -> ((i : Finset.Iic n) -> Reward) :=
925     fun omega => Preorder.frestrictLe n (reward omega)
926   let nextAmbient : Omega -> Reward := fun omega => reward omega (n + 1)
927   let prefixCanonical :
928       ((t : Nat) -> Reward) -> ((i : Finset.Iic n) -> Reward) :=
929     Preorder.frestrictLe n
930   let nextCanonical : ((t : Nat) -> Reward) -> Reward :=
931     fun trajectory => trajectory (n + 1)
932   let pairCanonical :
933       ((t : Nat) -> Reward) ->
934         (((i : Finset.Iic n) -> Reward) × Reward) :=
935     fun trajectory =>
936       (prefixCanonical trajectory, nextCanonical trajectory)
937   have hprefixAmbient : Measurable prefixAmbient := by
938     exact History.measurable_finiteRewardHistoryOfTrace reward hreward n
939   have hnextAmbient : Measurable nextAmbient := hreward (n + 1)
940   have hprefixCanonical : Measurable prefixCanonical := by
941     fun_prop
942   have hnextCanonical : Measurable nextCanonical :=
943     measurable_pi_apply (n + 1)
944   have hpairCanonical : Measurable pairCanonical :=
945     Measurable.prod hprefixCanonical hnextCanonical
946   have hident' :
947       ProbabilityTheory.IdentDistrib reward id mu trajMeasure := by
948     simpa [trajMeasure, stepKernel] using hident
949   have hprefixMap :
950       Measure.map prefixAmbient mu =
951         Measure.map prefixCanonical trajMeasure := by
952     simpa [prefixAmbient, prefixCanonical, Function.comp_def] using
953       (hident'.comp hprefixCanonical).map_eq
954   have hjointMap :
955       Measure.map (fun omega => (prefixAmbient omega, nextAmbient omega)) mu =
956         Measure.map pairCanonical trajMeasure := by
957     simpa [pairCanonical, prefixAmbient, nextAmbient, prefixCanonical,
958       nextCanonical, Function.comp_def] using
959       (hident'.comp hpairCanonical).map_eq
960   have hcanonicalCond :
961       Filter.EventuallyEq (ae (trajMeasure.map prefixCanonical))
962         (ProbabilityTheory.condDistrib nextCanonical prefixCanonical
963           trajMeasure)
964         (stepKernel n) := by
965     simpa [trajMeasure, stepKernel, prefixCanonical, nextCanonical] using
966       (ProbabilityTheory.Kernel.condDistrib_trajMeasure
967         (X := fun _ : Nat => Reward) (a := n))
968   have hcanonicalJoint :
969       Measure.map pairCanonical trajMeasure =
970         Measure.compProd (Measure.map prefixCanonical trajMeasure)
971           (stepKernel n) :=
972     (ProbabilityTheory.condDistrib_ae_eq_iff_measure_eq_compProd
973       prefixCanonical hnextCanonical.aemeasurable (stepKernel n)).mp
974         (by simpa [pairCanonical] using hcanonicalCond)
975   have hambientJoint :
976       Measure.map (fun omega => (prefixAmbient omega, nextAmbient omega)) mu =
977         Measure.compProd (Measure.map prefixAmbient mu) (stepKernel n) := by
978     calc
979       Measure.map (fun omega => (prefixAmbient omega, nextAmbient omega)) mu =
980           Measure.map pairCanonical trajMeasure := hjointMap
981       _ = Measure.compProd (Measure.map prefixCanonical trajMeasure)
982           (stepKernel n) :=
983         hcanonicalJoint
984       _ = Measure.compProd (Measure.map prefixAmbient mu) (stepKernel n) := by
985         rw [hprefixMap]
986   have hambientCond :
987       Filter.EventuallyEq (ae (mu.map prefixAmbient))
988         (ProbabilityTheory.condDistrib nextAmbient prefixAmbient mu)
989         (stepKernel n) :=
990     ProbabilityTheory.condDistrib_ae_eq_of_measure_eq_compProd_of_measurable
991       hprefixAmbient hnextAmbient hambientJoint
992   have hbridge :=
993     condExpKernel_map_eq_of_condDistrib_ae_eq_countable_trim
994       (mu := mu) (X := nextAmbient) (Y := prefixAmbient)
995       hnextAmbient hprefixAmbient (stepKernel n) hambientCond
996   simpa [stepKernel, prefixAmbient, nextAmbient,
997     RewardKernel.historyStepKernelFamily_apply] using hbridge
998 
999 /--
1000 Canonical `trajMeasure` next-pair law in `condExpKernel.map` form.
1001 
1002 This is the pair-coordinate analogue of
1003 `actionRewardHistoryStepKernelFamily_reward_condExpKernel_map_trajMeasure`: on
1004 Mathlib's canonical action/reward trajectory measure, conditioning on the
1005 finite pair prefix and pushing `condExpKernel` forward by the next pair
1006 coordinate recovers the configured history-step kernel.
1007 -/
1008 theorem actionRewardHistoryStepKernelFamily_pair_condExpKernel_map_trajMeasure
1009     {Context : Type x} {State : Type u} {Action : Type v}
1010     {Reward : Type w}
1011     [MeasurableSpace Context] [MeasurableSpace State]
1012     [MeasurableSpace Action] [MeasurableSpace Reward]
1013     [StandardBorelSpace (Prod Action Reward)]
1014     [StandardBorelSpace ((t : Nat) -> Prod Action Reward)]
1015     [Nonempty (Prod Action Reward)]
1016     [Nonempty ((t : Nat) -> Prod Action Reward)]
1017     [MeasurableSingletonClass (Prod Action Reward)]
1018     [Countable (Prod Action Reward)]
1019     (mu0 : Measure (Prod Action Reward))
1020     [MeasureTheory.IsProbabilityMeasure mu0]
1021     (rewardKernel : RewardKernel.MarkovRewardKernel (Prod Context Action) Reward)
1022     (policy : Nat -> Policy.MeasurablePolicy State Action)
1023     (context :
1024       (n : Nat) -> ((i : Finset.Iic n) -> Prod Action Reward) -> Context)
1025     (state :
1026       (n : Nat) -> ((i : Finset.Iic n) -> Prod Action Reward) -> State)
1027     (hcontext : forall n : Nat, Measurable (context n))
1028     (hstate : forall n : Nat, Measurable (state n))
1029     (n : Nat) :
1030     let stepKernel :=
1031       RewardKernel.actionRewardHistoryStepKernelFamily rewardKernel policy
1032         context state hcontext hstate
1033     let trajMeasure :=
1034       ProbabilityTheory.Kernel.trajMeasure
1035         (X := fun _ : Nat => Prod Action Reward) mu0 stepKernel
1036     Filter.Eventually
1037       (fun trajectory : (t : Nat) -> Prod Action Reward =>
1038         @Measure.map ((t : Nat) -> Prod Action Reward) (Prod Action Reward)
1039           inferInstance inferInstance
1040           (fun y : (t : Nat) -> Prod Action Reward => y (n + 1))
1041           (@ProbabilityTheory.condExpKernel
1042             ((t : Nat) -> Prod Action Reward) inferInstance _
1043             trajMeasure _
1044             ((inferInstance :
1045               MeasurableSpace
1046                 ((i : Finset.Iic n) -> Prod Action Reward)).comap
1047               (Preorder.frestrictLe n))
1048             trajectory) =
1049         (stepKernel n) (Preorder.frestrictLe n trajectory))
1050       (ae trajMeasure) := by
1051   let stepKernel :=
1052     RewardKernel.actionRewardHistoryStepKernelFamily rewardKernel policy
1053       context state hcontext hstate
1054   let trajMeasure :=
1055     ProbabilityTheory.Kernel.trajMeasure
1056       (X := fun _ : Nat => Prod Action Reward) mu0 stepKernel
1057   let prefixMap :
1058       ((t : Nat) -> Prod Action Reward) ->
1059         ((i : Finset.Iic n) -> Prod Action Reward) :=
1060     Preorder.frestrictLe n
1061   let nextPair : ((t : Nat) -> Prod Action Reward) -> Prod Action Reward :=
1062     fun trajectory => trajectory (n + 1)
1063   have h_nextPair : Measurable nextPair := by
1064     exact measurable_pi_apply (n + 1)
1065   have h_prefix : Measurable prefixMap := by
1066     fun_prop
1067   have hcond :
1068       Filter.EventuallyEq (ae (trajMeasure.map prefixMap))
1069         (ProbabilityTheory.condDistrib nextPair prefixMap trajMeasure)
1070         (stepKernel n) := by
1071     simpa [stepKernel, trajMeasure, prefixMap, nextPair] using
1072       RewardKernel.actionRewardHistoryStepKernelFamily_condDistrib_trajMeasure
1073         mu0 rewardKernel policy context state hcontext hstate n
1074   have hbridge :=
1075     condExpKernel_map_eq_of_condDistrib_ae_eq_countable
1076       (mu := trajMeasure)
1077       (X := nextPair)
1078       (Y := prefixMap)
1079       h_nextPair h_prefix (stepKernel n) hcond
1080   simpa [stepKernel, trajMeasure, prefixMap, nextPair] using hbridge

1082 /--
1083 Canonical `trajMeasure` next-action law in `condExpKernel.map` form.
1084 
1085 This is the action-coordinate projection of
1086 `actionRewardHistoryStepKernelFamily_pair_condExpKernel_map_trajMeasure`: on
1087 Mathlib's canonical action/reward trajectory measure, conditioning on the
1088 finite pair prefix and pushing `condExpKernel` forward by the next action
1089 coordinate recovers the Dirac law at the policy-selected action.
1090 -/
1091 theorem actionRewardHistoryStepKernelFamily_action_condExpKernel_map_trajMeasure
1092     {Context : Type x} {State : Type u} {Action : Type v}
1093     {Reward : Type w}
1094     [MeasurableSpace Context] [MeasurableSpace State]
1095     [MeasurableSpace Action] [MeasurableSpace Reward]
1096     [StandardBorelSpace (Prod Action Reward)]
1097     [StandardBorelSpace ((t : Nat) -> Prod Action Reward)]
1098     [Nonempty (Prod Action Reward)]
1099     [Nonempty ((t : Nat) -> Prod Action Reward)]
1100     [MeasurableSingletonClass (Prod Action Reward)]
1101     [Countable (Prod Action Reward)]
1102     (mu0 : Measure (Prod Action Reward))
1103     [MeasureTheory.IsProbabilityMeasure mu0]
1104     (rewardKernel : RewardKernel.MarkovRewardKernel (Prod Context Action) Reward)
1105     (policy : Nat -> Policy.MeasurablePolicy State Action)
1106     (context :
1107       (n : Nat) -> ((i : Finset.Iic n) -> Prod Action Reward) -> Context)
1108     (state :
1109       (n : Nat) -> ((i : Finset.Iic n) -> Prod Action Reward) -> State)
1110     (hcontext : forall n : Nat, Measurable (context n))
1111     (hstate : forall n : Nat, Measurable (state n))
1112     (n : Nat) :
1113     let stepKernel :=
1114       RewardKernel.actionRewardHistoryStepKernelFamily rewardKernel policy
1115         context state hcontext hstate
1116     let trajMeasure :=
1117       ProbabilityTheory.Kernel.trajMeasure
1118         (X := fun _ : Nat => Prod Action Reward) mu0 stepKernel
1119     Filter.Eventually
1120       (fun trajectory : (t : Nat) -> Prod Action Reward =>
1121         @Measure.map ((t : Nat) -> Prod Action Reward) Action
1122           inferInstance inferInstance
1123           (fun y : (t : Nat) -> Prod Action Reward => (y (n + 1)).1)
1124           (@ProbabilityTheory.condExpKernel
1125             ((t : Nat) -> Prod Action Reward) inferInstance _
1126             trajMeasure _
1127             ((inferInstance :
1128               MeasurableSpace
1129                 ((i : Finset.Iic n) -> Prod Action Reward)).comap
1130               (Preorder.frestrictLe n))
1131             trajectory) =
1132         Measure.dirac
1133           ((policy n).action (state n (Preorder.frestrictLe n trajectory))))
1134       (ae trajMeasure) := by
1135   let stepKernel :=
1136     RewardKernel.actionRewardHistoryStepKernelFamily rewardKernel policy
1137       context state hcontext hstate
1138   let trajMeasure :=
1139     ProbabilityTheory.Kernel.trajMeasure
1140       (X := fun _ : Nat => Prod Action Reward) mu0 stepKernel
1141   have hpair :=
1142     actionRewardHistoryStepKernelFamily_pair_condExpKernel_map_trajMeasure
1143       mu0 rewardKernel policy context state hcontext hstate n
1144   filter_upwards [hpair] with trajectory h_pair
1145   let condKernel : Measure ((t : Nat) -> Prod Action Reward) :=
1146     @ProbabilityTheory.condExpKernel
1147       ((t : Nat) -> Prod Action Reward) inferInstance _ trajMeasure _
1148       ((inferInstance :
1149         MeasurableSpace ((i : Finset.Iic n) -> Prod Action Reward)).comap
1150         (Preorder.frestrictLe n))
1151       trajectory
1152   let nextPair : ((t : Nat) -> Prod Action Reward) -> Prod Action Reward :=
1153     fun y => y (n + 1)
1154   let nextAction : ((t : Nat) -> Prod Action Reward) -> Action :=
1155     fun y => (y (n + 1)).1
1156   have h_nextPair_meas : Measurable nextPair := by
1157     exact measurable_pi_apply (n + 1)
1158   calc
1159     @Measure.map ((t : Nat) -> Prod Action Reward) Action
1160         inferInstance inferInstance nextAction condKernel =
1161       Measure.map Prod.fst (Measure.map nextPair condKernel) := by
1162         rw [Measure.map_map measurable_fst h_nextPair_meas]
1163         rfl
1164     _ =
1165       Measure.map Prod.fst
1166         (stepKernel n (Preorder.frestrictLe n trajectory)) := by
1167         exact congrArg (Measure.map Prod.fst) h_pair
1168     _ =
1169       Measure.dirac
1170         ((policy n).action
1171           (state n (Preorder.frestrictLe n trajectory))) := by
1172         exact
1173           RewardKernel.actionRewardHistoryStepKernelFamily_action_map
1174             rewardKernel policy context state hcontext hstate n
1175             (Preorder.frestrictLe n trajectory)

1177 /--
1178 Canonical `trajMeasure` action-marginal law in `condExpKernel.map` form via
1179 the action-coordinate `condDistrib` route.
1180 
1181 This is the action-side analogue of
1182 `actionRewardHistoryStepKernelFamily_reward_condExpKernel_map_trajMeasure`:
1183 on the canonical action/reward trajectory measure, conditioning on the finite
1184 pair prefix and pushing `condExpKernel` forward by the next action coordinate
1185 recovers the `Prod.fst` marginal of the configured history-step kernel.  The

1220           inferInstance inferInstance

1224             trajMeasure _

1228               (Preorder.frestrictLe n))

1243     fun trajectory => (trajectory (n + 1)).1
1244   have h_nextAction : Measurable nextAction := by
1245     exact measurable_fst.comp (measurable_pi_apply (n + 1))
1246   have h_prefix : Measurable prefixMap := by

1257       (mu := trajMeasure)

1349 /--
1350 Canonical `trajMeasure` selected-action law in conditional a.e. form.
1351 
1352 The previous theorem identifies the pushforward of the conditional kernel by
1353 the next action coordinate as a Dirac law.  This wrapper turns that Dirac
1354 pushforward equality into the `Filter.EventuallyEq` shape required by the
1355 next-pair split-law route.
1356 -/
1357 theorem actionRewardHistoryStepKernelFamily_selectedAction_condExpKernel_ae_trajMeasure
1358     {Context : Type x} {State : Type u} {Action : Type v}
1359     {Reward : Type w}
1360     [MeasurableSpace Context] [MeasurableSpace State]
1361     [MeasurableSpace Action] [MeasurableSpace Reward]
1362     [StandardBorelSpace (Prod Action Reward)]
1363     [StandardBorelSpace Action]
1364     [StandardBorelSpace ((t : Nat) -> Prod Action Reward)]
1365     [Nonempty (Prod Action Reward)] [Nonempty Action]
1366     [Nonempty ((t : Nat) -> Prod Action Reward)]
1367     [MeasurableSingletonClass Action] [Countable Action]
1368     (mu0 : Measure (Prod Action Reward))
1369     [MeasureTheory.IsProbabilityMeasure mu0]
1370     (rewardKernel : RewardKernel.MarkovRewardKernel (Prod Context Action) Reward)
1371     (policy : Nat -> Policy.MeasurablePolicy State Action)
1372     (context :
1373       (n : Nat) -> ((i : Finset.Iic n) -> Prod Action Reward) -> Context)
1374     (state :
1375       (n : Nat) -> ((i : Finset.Iic n) -> Prod Action Reward) -> State)
1376     (hcontext : forall n : Nat, Measurable (context n))
1377     (hstate : forall n : Nat, Measurable (state n))
1378     (n : Nat) :
1379     let stepKernel :=
1380       RewardKernel.actionRewardHistoryStepKernelFamily rewardKernel policy
1381         context state hcontext hstate
1382     let trajMeasure :=
1383       ProbabilityTheory.Kernel.trajMeasure
1384         (X := fun _ : Nat => Prod Action Reward) mu0 stepKernel
1385     Filter.Eventually
1386       (fun trajectory : (t : Nat) -> Prod Action Reward =>
1387         Filter.EventuallyEq
1388           (ae
1389             (@ProbabilityTheory.condExpKernel
1390               ((t : Nat) -> Prod Action Reward) inferInstance _
1391               trajMeasure _
1392               ((inferInstance :
1393                 MeasurableSpace
1394                   ((i : Finset.Iic n) -> Prod Action Reward)).comap
1395                 (Preorder.frestrictLe n))
1396               trajectory))
1397           (fun y : (t : Nat) -> Prod Action Reward => (y (n + 1)).1)
1398           (fun _y : (t : Nat) -> Prod Action Reward =>
1399             (policy n).action (state n (Preorder.frestrictLe n trajectory))))
1400       (ae trajMeasure) := by
1401   let stepKernel :=
1402     RewardKernel.actionRewardHistoryStepKernelFamily rewardKernel policy
1403       context state hcontext hstate
1404   let trajMeasure :=
1405     ProbabilityTheory.Kernel.trajMeasure
1406       (X := fun _ : Nat => Prod Action Reward) mu0 stepKernel
1407   have hmap :=
1408     actionRewardHistoryStepKernelFamily_selectedAction_condExpKernel_map_trajMeasure
1409       mu0 rewardKernel policy context state hcontext hstate n
1410   filter_upwards [hmap] with trajectory h_map
1411   let condKernel : Measure ((t : Nat) -> Prod Action Reward) :=
1412     @ProbabilityTheory.condExpKernel
1413       ((t : Nat) -> Prod Action Reward) inferInstance _ trajMeasure _
1414       ((inferInstance :
1415         MeasurableSpace ((i : Finset.Iic n) -> Prod Action Reward)).comap
1416         (Preorder.frestrictLe n))
1417       trajectory
1418   let nextAction : ((t : Nat) -> Prod Action Reward) -> Action :=
1419     fun y : (t : Nat) -> Prod Action Reward => (y (n + 1)).1
1420   let selectedAction : Action :=
1421     (policy n).action (state n (Preorder.frestrictLe n trajectory))
1422   have h_nextAction : Measurable nextAction := by
1423     exact measurable_fst.comp (measurable_pi_apply (n + 1))
1424   exact
1425     eventuallyEq_const_of_map_eq_dirac
1426       condKernel nextAction selectedAction h_nextAction
1427       (by simpa [condKernel, nextAction, selectedAction, trajMeasure] using
1428         h_map)

1430 /--
1431 Canonical `trajMeasure` extension-map law in `condExpKernel.map` form.
1432 
1433 This pushes
1434 `actionRewardHistoryStepKernelFamily_pair_condExpKernel_map_trajMeasure`
1435 through `History.extendPairHistorySucc`, yielding the one-step
1436 `RewardKernel.actionRewardPartialTrajectoryKernel` surface used by the
1437 extension-map consumers.  It remains a canonical `trajMeasure` theorem; it does
1438 not transport an arbitrary ambient process.
1439 -/
1440 theorem actionRewardPartialTrajectoryKernel_extend_condExpKernel_map_trajMeasure
1441     {Context : Type x} {State : Type u} {Action : Type v}
1442     {Reward : Type w}
1443     [MeasurableSpace Context] [MeasurableSpace State]
1444     [MeasurableSpace Action] [MeasurableSpace Reward]
1445     [StandardBorelSpace (Prod Action Reward)]
1446     [StandardBorelSpace ((t : Nat) -> Prod Action Reward)]
1447     [Nonempty (Prod Action Reward)]
1448     [Nonempty ((t : Nat) -> Prod Action Reward)]
1449     [MeasurableSingletonClass (Prod Action Reward)]
1450     [Countable (Prod Action Reward)]
1451     (mu0 : Measure (Prod Action Reward))
1452     [MeasureTheory.IsProbabilityMeasure mu0]
1453     (rewardKernel : RewardKernel.MarkovRewardKernel (Prod Context Action) Reward)
1454     (policy : Nat -> Policy.MeasurablePolicy State Action)
1455     (context :
1456       (n : Nat) -> ((i : Finset.Iic n) -> Prod Action Reward) -> Context)
1457     (state :
1458       (n : Nat) -> ((i : Finset.Iic n) -> Prod Action Reward) -> State)
1459     (hcontext : forall n : Nat, Measurable (context n))
1460     (hstate : forall n : Nat, Measurable (state n))
1461     (n : Nat) :
1462     let stepKernel :=
1463       RewardKernel.actionRewardHistoryStepKernelFamily rewardKernel policy
1464         context state hcontext hstate
1465     let trajMeasure :=
1466       ProbabilityTheory.Kernel.trajMeasure
1467         (X := fun _ : Nat => Prod Action Reward) mu0 stepKernel
1468     Filter.Eventually
1469       (fun trajectory : (t : Nat) -> Prod Action Reward =>
1470         @Measure.map
1471           ((t : Nat) -> Prod Action Reward)
1472           ((i : Finset.Iic (n + 1)) -> Prod Action Reward)
1473           inferInstance inferInstance
1474           (fun y : (t : Nat) -> Prod Action Reward =>
1475             History.extendPairHistorySucc
1476               (Preorder.frestrictLe n trajectory)
1477               (y (n + 1)))
1478           (@ProbabilityTheory.condExpKernel
1479             ((t : Nat) -> Prod Action Reward) inferInstance _
1480             trajMeasure _
1481             ((inferInstance :
1482               MeasurableSpace
1483                 ((i : Finset.Iic n) -> Prod Action Reward)).comap
1484               (Preorder.frestrictLe n))
1485             trajectory) =
1486         RewardKernel.actionRewardPartialTrajectoryKernel rewardKernel policy
1487           context state hcontext hstate n (n + 1)
1488           (Preorder.frestrictLe n trajectory))
1489       (ae trajMeasure) := by
1490   let stepKernel :=
1491     RewardKernel.actionRewardHistoryStepKernelFamily rewardKernel policy
1492       context state hcontext hstate
1493   let trajMeasure :=
1494     ProbabilityTheory.Kernel.trajMeasure
1495       (X := fun _ : Nat => Prod Action Reward) mu0 stepKernel
1496   have hpair :=
1497     actionRewardHistoryStepKernelFamily_pair_condExpKernel_map_trajMeasure
1498       mu0 rewardKernel policy context state hcontext hstate n
1499   filter_upwards [hpair] with trajectory h_pair
1500   let condKernel : Measure ((t : Nat) -> Prod Action Reward) :=
1501     @ProbabilityTheory.condExpKernel
1502       ((t : Nat) -> Prod Action Reward) inferInstance _ trajMeasure _
1503       ((inferInstance :
1504         MeasurableSpace ((i : Finset.Iic n) -> Prod Action Reward)).comap
1505         (Preorder.frestrictLe n))
1506       trajectory
1507   let histPrefix : (i : Finset.Iic n) -> Prod Action Reward :=
1508     Preorder.frestrictLe n trajectory
1509   let nextPair : ((t : Nat) -> Prod Action Reward) -> Prod Action Reward :=
1510     fun y => y (n + 1)
1511   let extendNext :
1512       Prod Action Reward -> ((i : Finset.Iic (n + 1)) -> Prod Action Reward) :=
1513     fun next => History.extendPairHistorySucc histPrefix next
1514   have h_nextPair_meas : Measurable nextPair := by
1515     exact measurable_pi_apply (n + 1)
1516   have h_extend_input_meas :
1517       Measurable
1518         (fun next : Prod Action Reward =>
1519           (histPrefix, next)) := by
1520     fun_prop
1521   have h_extendNext_meas : Measurable extendNext := by
1522     simpa [extendNext] using
1523       (History.measurable_extendPairHistorySucc
1524         (Action := Action) (Reward := Reward) (t := n)).comp
1525         h_extend_input_meas
1526   have h_extend_map :
1527       Measure.map extendNext (Measure.map nextPair condKernel) =
1528         @Measure.map
1529           ((t : Nat) -> Prod Action Reward)
1530           ((i : Finset.Iic (n + 1)) -> Prod Action Reward)
1531           inferInstance inferInstance
1532           (fun y : (t : Nat) -> Prod Action Reward =>
1533             History.extendPairHistorySucc
1534               (Preorder.frestrictLe n trajectory)
1535               (y (n + 1)))
1536           condKernel := by
1537     rw [Measure.map_map h_extendNext_meas h_nextPair_meas]
1538     rfl
1539   calc
1540     @Measure.map
1541         ((t : Nat) -> Prod Action Reward)
1542         ((i : Finset.Iic (n + 1)) -> Prod Action Reward)
1543         inferInstance inferInstance
1544         (fun y : (t : Nat) -> Prod Action Reward =>
1545           History.extendPairHistorySucc
1546             (Preorder.frestrictLe n trajectory)
1547             (y (n + 1)))
1548         (@ProbabilityTheory.condExpKernel
1549           ((t : Nat) -> Prod Action Reward) inferInstance _
1550           trajMeasure _
1551           ((inferInstance :
1552             MeasurableSpace
1553               ((i : Finset.Iic n) -> Prod Action Reward)).comap
1554             (Preorder.frestrictLe n))
1555           trajectory)
1556         =
1557       Measure.map extendNext (Measure.map nextPair condKernel) := by
1558         simpa [condKernel, histPrefix, nextPair, extendNext] using h_extend_map.symm
1559     _ =
1560       Measure.map extendNext
1561         (stepKernel n (Preorder.frestrictLe n trajectory)) := by
1562         simpa [condKernel, histPrefix, nextPair, stepKernel] using
1563           congrArg (Measure.map extendNext) h_pair
1564     _ =
1565       RewardKernel.actionRewardPartialTrajectoryKernel rewardKernel policy
1566         context state hcontext hstate n (n + 1)
1567         (Preorder.frestrictLe n trajectory) := by
1568         simpa [extendNext, histPrefix, stepKernel] using
1569           (RewardKernel.actionRewardPartialTrajectoryKernel_succ_extend_map_apply
1570             rewardKernel policy context state hcontext hstate n
1571             (Preorder.frestrictLe n trajectory)).symm
1572 
1573 /--
1574 Canonical `trajMeasure` reward law in `condExpKernel.map` form.
1575 
1576 This specializes `condExpKernel_map_eq_of_condDistrib_ae_eq_countable` to the
1577 Mathlib Ionescu-Tulcea trajectory measure generated by
1578 `RewardKernel.actionRewardHistoryStepKernelFamily`.  It converts the canonical
1579 next-reward `condDistrib` law into the `condExpKernel` pushforward-map shape
1580 used by the project-local conditional reward consumers.

1582 theorem actionRewardHistoryStepKernelFamily_reward_condExpKernel_map_trajMeasure
1583     {Context : Type x} {State : Type u} {Action : Type v}
1584     {Reward : Type w}
1585     [MeasurableSpace Context] [MeasurableSpace State]
1586     [MeasurableSpace Action] [MeasurableSpace Reward]
1587     [StandardBorelSpace (Prod Action Reward)]
1588     [StandardBorelSpace Reward]
1589     [StandardBorelSpace ((t : Nat) -> Prod Action Reward)]
1590     [Nonempty (Prod Action Reward)] [Nonempty Reward]
1591     [Nonempty ((t : Nat) -> Prod Action Reward)]
1592     [MeasurableSingletonClass Reward] [Countable Reward]
1593     (mu0 : Measure (Prod Action Reward))
1594     [MeasureTheory.IsProbabilityMeasure mu0]
1595     (rewardKernel : RewardKernel.MarkovRewardKernel (Prod Context Action) Reward)
1596     (policy : Nat -> Policy.MeasurablePolicy State Action)
1597     (context :
1598       (n : Nat) -> ((i : Finset.Iic n) -> Prod Action Reward) -> Context)
1599     (state :
1600       (n : Nat) -> ((i : Finset.Iic n) -> Prod Action Reward) -> State)
1601     (hcontext : forall n : Nat, Measurable (context n))
1602     (hstate : forall n : Nat, Measurable (state n))
1603     (n : Nat) :
1604     let stepKernel :=
1605       RewardKernel.actionRewardHistoryStepKernelFamily rewardKernel policy
1606         context state hcontext hstate
1607     let trajMeasure :=
1608       ProbabilityTheory.Kernel.trajMeasure
1609         (X := fun _ : Nat => Prod Action Reward) mu0 stepKernel
1610     Filter.Eventually
1611       (fun trajectory : (t : Nat) -> Prod Action Reward =>
1612         @Measure.map ((t : Nat) -> Prod Action Reward) Reward
1613           inferInstance inferInstance
1614           (fun y : (t : Nat) -> Prod Action Reward => (y (n + 1)).2)
1615           (@ProbabilityTheory.condExpKernel
1616             ((t : Nat) -> Prod Action Reward) inferInstance _
1617             trajMeasure _
1618             ((inferInstance :
1619               MeasurableSpace
1620                 ((i : Finset.Iic n) -> Prod Action Reward)).comap
1621               (Preorder.frestrictLe n))
1622             trajectory) =
1623         ((stepKernel n).map Prod.snd) (Preorder.frestrictLe n trajectory))
1624       (ae trajMeasure) := by
1625   let stepKernel :=
1626     RewardKernel.actionRewardHistoryStepKernelFamily rewardKernel policy
1627       context state hcontext hstate
1628   let trajMeasure :=
1629     ProbabilityTheory.Kernel.trajMeasure
1630       (X := fun _ : Nat => Prod Action Reward) mu0 stepKernel
1631   let prefixMap :
1632       ((t : Nat) -> Prod Action Reward) ->
1633         ((i : Finset.Iic n) -> Prod Action Reward) :=
1634     Preorder.frestrictLe n
1635   let nextReward : ((t : Nat) -> Prod Action Reward) -> Reward :=
1636     fun trajectory => (trajectory (n + 1)).2
1637   have h_nextReward : Measurable nextReward := by
1638     exact measurable_snd.comp (measurable_pi_apply (n + 1))
1639   have h_prefix : Measurable prefixMap := by
1640     fun_prop
1641   have hcond :
1642       Filter.EventuallyEq (ae (trajMeasure.map prefixMap))
1643         (ProbabilityTheory.condDistrib nextReward prefixMap trajMeasure)
1644         ((stepKernel n).map Prod.snd) := by
1645     simpa [stepKernel, trajMeasure, prefixMap, nextReward] using
1646       RewardKernel.actionRewardHistoryStepKernelFamily_reward_condDistrib_trajMeasure
1647         mu0 rewardKernel policy context state hcontext hstate n
1648   have hbridge :=
1649     condExpKernel_map_eq_of_condDistrib_ae_eq_countable
1650       (mu := trajMeasure)
1651       (X := nextReward)
1652       (Y := prefixMap)
1653       h_nextReward h_prefix ((stepKernel n).map Prod.snd) hcond
1654   simpa [stepKernel, trajMeasure, prefixMap, nextReward] using hbridge

1909 /--
1910 Canonical `trajMeasure` next-pair law rebuilt from split inputs.
1911 
1912 This theorem verifies the split route on Mathlib's canonical
1913 Ionescu-Tulcea trajectory measure: the selected-action conditional a.e. law
1914 and the selected-reward pushforward law combine to recover the full
1915 `RewardKernel.actionRewardHistoryStepKernelFamily` next-pair law.  Unlike the
1916 direct pair-coordinate `condDistrib` bridge, this route only needs separate
1917 countability of `Action` and `Reward`.

1919 theorem actionRewardHistoryStepKernelFamily_pair_condExpKernel_map_trajMeasure_of_selectedAction_ae_selectedMeasure
1920     {Context : Type x} {State : Type u} {Action : Type v}
1921     {Reward : Type w}
1922     [MeasurableSpace Context] [MeasurableSpace State]
1923     [MeasurableSpace Action] [MeasurableSpace Reward]
1924     [StandardBorelSpace (Prod Action Reward)]
1925     [StandardBorelSpace Action] [StandardBorelSpace Reward]
1926     [StandardBorelSpace ((t : Nat) -> Prod Action Reward)]
1927     [Nonempty (Prod Action Reward)] [Nonempty Action] [Nonempty Reward]
1928     [Nonempty ((t : Nat) -> Prod Action Reward)]
1929     [MeasurableSingletonClass Action] [Countable Action]
1930     [MeasurableSingletonClass Reward] [Countable Reward]
1931     (mu0 : Measure (Prod Action Reward))
1932     [MeasureTheory.IsProbabilityMeasure mu0]
1933     (rewardKernel : RewardKernel.MarkovRewardKernel (Prod Context Action) Reward)
1934     (policy : Nat -> Policy.MeasurablePolicy State Action)
1935     (context :
1936       (n : Nat) -> ((i : Finset.Iic n) -> Prod Action Reward) -> Context)
1937     (state :
1938       (n : Nat) -> ((i : Finset.Iic n) -> Prod Action Reward) -> State)
1939     (hcontext : forall n : Nat, Measurable (context n))
1940     (hstate : forall n : Nat, Measurable (state n))
1941     (n : Nat) :
1942     let stepKernel :=
1943       RewardKernel.actionRewardHistoryStepKernelFamily rewardKernel policy
1944         context state hcontext hstate
1945     let trajMeasure :=
1946       ProbabilityTheory.Kernel.trajMeasure
1947         (X := fun _ : Nat => Prod Action Reward) mu0 stepKernel
1948     Filter.Eventually
1949       (fun trajectory : (t : Nat) -> Prod Action Reward =>
1950         @Measure.map ((t : Nat) -> Prod Action Reward) (Prod Action Reward)
1951           inferInstance inferInstance
1952           (fun y : (t : Nat) -> Prod Action Reward => y (n + 1))
1953           (@ProbabilityTheory.condExpKernel
1954             ((t : Nat) -> Prod Action Reward) inferInstance _
1955             trajMeasure _
1956             ((inferInstance :
1957               MeasurableSpace
1958                 ((i : Finset.Iic n) -> Prod Action Reward)).comap
1959               (Preorder.frestrictLe n))
1960             trajectory) =
1961         stepKernel n (Preorder.frestrictLe n trajectory))
1962       (ae trajMeasure) := by
1963   let stepKernel :=
1964     RewardKernel.actionRewardHistoryStepKernelFamily rewardKernel policy
1965       context state hcontext hstate
1966   let trajMeasure :=
1967     ProbabilityTheory.Kernel.trajMeasure
1968       (X := fun _ : Nat => Prod Action Reward) mu0 stepKernel
1969   have haction :=
1970     actionRewardHistoryStepKernelFamily_selectedAction_condExpKernel_ae_trajMeasure
1971       mu0 rewardKernel policy context state hcontext hstate n
1972   have hreward :=
1973     actionRewardHistoryStepKernelFamily_selectedMeasure_condExpKernel_map_trajMeasure
1974       mu0 rewardKernel policy context state hcontext hstate n
1975   filter_upwards [haction, hreward] with trajectory h_action h_reward
1976   let condKernel : Measure ((t : Nat) -> Prod Action Reward) :=
1977     @ProbabilityTheory.condExpKernel
1978       ((t : Nat) -> Prod Action Reward) inferInstance _ trajMeasure _
1979       ((inferInstance :
1980         MeasurableSpace ((i : Finset.Iic n) -> Prod Action Reward)).comap
1981         (Preorder.frestrictLe n))
1982       trajectory
1983   let nextAction : ((t : Nat) -> Prod Action Reward) -> Action :=
1984     fun y : (t : Nat) -> Prod Action Reward => (y (n + 1)).1
1985   let nextReward : ((t : Nat) -> Prod Action Reward) -> Reward :=
1986     fun y : (t : Nat) -> Prod Action Reward => (y (n + 1)).2
1987   let selectedAction : Action :=
1988     (policy n).action (state n (Preorder.frestrictLe n trajectory))
1989   let selectedReward : Measure Reward :=
1990     RewardKernel.selectedMeasure rewardKernel
1991       (context n (Preorder.frestrictLe n trajectory)) selectedAction
1992   have h_nextReward : Measurable nextReward := by
1993     exact measurable_snd.comp (measurable_pi_apply (n + 1))
1994   have hsplit :
1995       @Measure.map ((t : Nat) -> Prod Action Reward) (Prod Action Reward)
1996           inferInstance inferInstance
1997           (fun y : (t : Nat) -> Prod Action Reward =>
1998             (nextAction y, nextReward y)) condKernel =
1999         Measure.map (Prod.mk selectedAction) selectedReward := by
2000     exact
2001       pair_map_eq_map_prod_mk_of_action_ae_eq_const_reward_map_eq
2002         condKernel nextAction nextReward selectedAction selectedReward
2003         h_nextReward h_action
2004         (by
2005           simpa [condKernel, nextReward, selectedReward, selectedAction,
2006             trajMeasure] using h_reward)
2007   calc
2008     @Measure.map ((t : Nat) -> Prod Action Reward) (Prod Action Reward)
2009         inferInstance inferInstance
2010         (fun y : (t : Nat) -> Prod Action Reward => y (n + 1))
2011         (@ProbabilityTheory.condExpKernel
2012           ((t : Nat) -> Prod Action Reward) inferInstance _ trajMeasure _
2013           ((inferInstance :
2014             MeasurableSpace
2015               ((i : Finset.Iic n) -> Prod Action Reward)).comap
2016             (Preorder.frestrictLe n))
2017           trajectory)
2018         =
2019       Measure.map (Prod.mk selectedAction) selectedReward := by
2020         simpa [condKernel, nextAction, nextReward] using hsplit
2021     _ = stepKernel n (Preorder.frestrictLe n trajectory) := by
2022         simpa [stepKernel, selectedAction, selectedReward] using
2023           (RewardKernel.actionRewardHistoryStepKernelFamily_apply_eq_map_prod_mk
2024             rewardKernel policy context state hcontext hstate n
2025             (Preorder.frestrictLe n trajectory)).symm
