2027 /--
2028 Generic `condExpKernel` map-law consumer for conditional sub-Gaussianity.
2029 
2030 If the conditional kernel pushed forward by `X` is trim-a.e. a target measure
2031 whose identity random variable is sub-Gaussian with deterministic proxy `c`,
2032 then `X` is conditionally sub-Gaussian.  The target MGF bound also supplies the
2033 global exponential-integrability field: `Measure.integrable_comp_iff` combines
2034 the target laws' pointwise integrability with their common deterministic MGF
2035 bound over the finite trim measure.
2036 -/
2037 theorem hasCondSubgaussianMGF_of_condExpKernel_map_eq
2038     {Omega : Type u}
2039     [mOmega : MeasurableSpace Omega] [StandardBorelSpace Omega]
2040     (mu : Measure Omega) [IsFiniteMeasure mu]
2041     (mcond : MeasurableSpace Omega) (hm : mcond <= mOmega)
2042     (X : Omega -> Real) (c : NNReal)
2043     (hX : @Measurable Omega Real mOmega inferInstance X)
2044     (target : Omega -> Measure Real)
2045     (h_kernel_map_eq :
2046       Filter.Eventually
2047         (fun omega : Omega =>
2048           @Measure.map Omega Real mOmega inferInstance X
2049             (@ProbabilityTheory.condExpKernel Omega mOmega _ mu _ mcond
2050               omega) =
2051           target omega)
2052         (ae (mu.trim hm)))
2053     (h_target_subG :
2054       Filter.Eventually
2055         (fun omega : Omega =>
2056           ProbabilityTheory.HasSubgaussianMGF
2057             (fun z : Real => z) c (target omega))
2058         (ae (mu.trim hm))) :
2059     ProbabilityTheory.HasCondSubgaussianMGF mcond hm X c mu := by
2060   let condKernel :=
2061     @ProbabilityTheory.condExpKernel Omega mOmega _ mu _ mcond
2062   have h_cond_subG :
2063       Filter.Eventually
2064         (fun omega : Omega =>
2065           ProbabilityTheory.HasSubgaussianMGF X c (condKernel omega))
2066         (ae (mu.trim hm)) := by
2067     filter_upwards [h_kernel_map_eq, h_target_subG] with omega hmap hsub
2068     apply
2069       (ProbabilityTheory.HasSubgaussianMGF.id_map_iff
2070         hX.aemeasurable).1
2071     rw [hmap]
2072     exact hsub
2073   have h_integrable_comp :
2074       forall t : Real,
2075         Integrable (fun omega : Omega => Real.exp (t * X omega))
2076           ((mu.trim hm).bind condKernel) := by
2077     intro t
2078     let f : Omega -> Real := fun omega => Real.exp (t * X omega)
2079     have hf : @Measurable Omega Real mOmega inferInstance f := by
2080       exact Real.measurable_exp.comp (measurable_const.mul hX)
2081     refine
2082       (@Measure.integrable_comp_iff Omega Omega Real mcond mOmega _
2083         condKernel (mu.trim hm) f hf.aestronglyMeasurable).2 ⟨?_, ?_⟩
2084     · filter_upwards [h_cond_subG] with omega hsub
2085       simpa [f] using hsub.integrable_exp_mul t
2086     · have h_inner_strong :
2087           @StronglyMeasurable Omega Real inferInstance mcond
2088             (fun omega : Omega =>
2089               ∫ y, ‖f y‖ ∂condKernel omega) :=
2090         (@StronglyMeasurable.integral_kernel Omega Omega mcond mOmega
2091           condKernel Real _ _ (fun y : Omega => ‖f y‖)
2092           hf.stronglyMeasurable.norm)
2093       refine Integrable.of_bound h_inner_strong.aestronglyMeasurable
2094         (Real.exp (((c : NNReal) : Real) * t ^ 2 / 2)) ?_
2095       filter_upwards [h_cond_subG] with omega hsub
2096       have h_inner_eq :
2097           (∫ y, ‖f y‖ ∂condKernel omega) =
2098             ProbabilityTheory.mgf X (condKernel omega) t := by
2099         simp [f, ProbabilityTheory.mgf, Real.norm_eq_abs]
2100       rw [h_inner_eq,
2101         Real.norm_of_nonneg ProbabilityTheory.mgf_nonneg]
2102       exact hsub.mgf_le t
2103   change
2104     ProbabilityTheory.Kernel.HasSubgaussianMGF X c condKernel (mu.trim hm)
2105   refine ProbabilityTheory.Kernel.HasSubgaussianMGF.of_rat
2106     h_integrable_comp ?_
2107   intro q
2108   filter_upwards [h_cond_subG] with omega hsub
2109   simpa using hsub.mgf_le (q : Real)
2110 
2111 /--
2112 Center a real-valued conditional reward law by a quantity measurable in the
2113 conditioning sigma-algebra.
2114 
2115 The conditional kernel freezes the center at the conditioning point.  The
2116 resulting residual pushforward is therefore the centered pushforward of the
2117 supplied reward law, which can be consumed by the generic conditional
2118 sub-Gaussian map-law theorem.
2119 -/
2120 theorem hasCondSubgaussianMGF_centered_of_condExpKernel_map_eq
2121     {Omega : Type u}
2122     [mOmega : MeasurableSpace Omega] [StandardBorelSpace Omega]
2123     (mu : Measure Omega) [IsFiniteMeasure mu]
2124     (mcond : MeasurableSpace Omega) (hm : mcond <= mOmega)
2125     (X center : Omega -> Real) (c : NNReal)
2126     (hX : @Measurable Omega Real mOmega inferInstance X)
2127     (hcenter : @Measurable Omega Real mcond inferInstance center)
2128     (target : Omega -> Measure Real)
2129     (h_kernel_map_eq :
2130       Filter.Eventually
2131         (fun omega : Omega =>
2132           @Measure.map Omega Real mOmega inferInstance X
2133             (@ProbabilityTheory.condExpKernel Omega mOmega _ mu _ mcond
2134               omega) =
2135           target omega)
2136         (ae (mu.trim hm)))
2137     (h_target_subG :
2138       Filter.Eventually
2139         (fun omega : Omega =>
2140           ProbabilityTheory.HasSubgaussianMGF
2141             (fun z : Real => z - center omega) c (target omega))
2142         (ae (mu.trim hm))) :
2143     ProbabilityTheory.HasCondSubgaussianMGF mcond hm
2144       (fun omega => X omega - center omega) c mu := by
2145   let condKernel :=
2146     @ProbabilityTheory.condExpKernel Omega mOmega _ mu _ mcond
2147   have hcenter_map :=
2148     condExpKernel_map_eq_dirac_of_measurable
2149       (mOmega := mOmega) mu mcond hm center hcenter
2150   have h_residual_map :
2151       Filter.Eventually
2152         (fun omega : Omega =>
2153           @Measure.map Omega Real mOmega inferInstance
2154               (fun y => X y - center y) (condKernel omega) =
2155             Measure.map (fun z : Real => z - center omega)
2156               (target omega))
2157         (ae (mu.trim hm)) := by
2158     filter_upwards [h_kernel_map_eq, hcenter_map] with omega hreward hcenter_eq
2159     have hcenter_ae :
2160         Filter.EventuallyEq (ae (condKernel omega)) center
2161           (fun _y : Omega => center omega) :=
2162       eventuallyEq_const_of_map_eq_dirac
2163         (mOmega := mOmega) (condKernel omega) center (center omega)
2164         (hcenter.mono hm le_rfl) hcenter_eq
2165     calc
2166       @Measure.map Omega Real mOmega inferInstance
2167           (fun y => X y - center y) (condKernel omega) =
2168           @Measure.map Omega Real mOmega inferInstance
2169             (fun y => X y - center omega) (condKernel omega) := by
2170             apply Measure.map_congr
2171             filter_upwards [hcenter_ae] with y hy
2172             rw [hy]
2173       _ = Measure.map (fun z : Real => z - center omega)
2174           (@Measure.map Omega Real mOmega inferInstance X
2175             (condKernel omega)) := by
2176             symm
2177             simpa [Function.comp_def] using
2178               (Measure.map_map
2179                 (measurable_id.sub_const (center omega)) hX)
2180       _ = Measure.map (fun z : Real => z - center omega)
2181           (target omega) := by rw [hreward]
2182   apply hasCondSubgaussianMGF_of_condExpKernel_map_eq (mOmega := mOmega)
2183     mu mcond hm (fun omega => X omega - center omega) c
2184     (hX.sub (hcenter.mono hm le_rfl))
2185     (fun omega =>
2186       Measure.map (fun z : Real => z - center omega) (target omega))
2187     h_residual_map
2188   filter_upwards [h_target_subG] with omega hsub
2189   exact
2190     (ProbabilityTheory.HasSubgaussianMGF.id_map_iff
2191       (measurable_id.sub_const (center omega)).aemeasurable).2 hsub
2192 
2193 /--
2194 Transport conditional sub-Gaussianity across equal conditioning measurable
2195 spaces.  The two inclusion proofs are propositionally irrelevant.
2196 -/
2197 theorem hasCondSubgaussianMGF_congr_measurableSpace
2198     {Omega : Type u}
2199     [mOmega : MeasurableSpace Omega] [StandardBorelSpace Omega]
2200     (mu : Measure Omega) [IsFiniteMeasure mu]
2201     (mcond mcond' : MeasurableSpace Omega)
2202     (hm : mcond <= mOmega) (hm' : mcond' <= mOmega)
2203     (hspaces : mcond = mcond')
2204     (X : Omega -> Real) (c : NNReal)
2205     (h :
2206       ProbabilityTheory.HasCondSubgaussianMGF
2207         (mΩ := mOmega) mcond hm X c mu) :
2208     ProbabilityTheory.HasCondSubgaussianMGF
2209       (mΩ := mOmega) mcond' hm' X c mu := by
2210   subst mcond'
2211   simpa only using h

2213 /--
2214 Centered-reward specialization of
2215 `condExp_eq_zero_of_condExpKernel_integral_eq_zero`.
2216 
2217 The statement matches the succ-indexed shape used by Mathlib's conditional
2218 tail API: the reward at `i + 1` is conditioned on filtration level `i`.
2219 -/
2220 theorem centeredReward_succ_condExp_eq_zero_of_condExpKernel_integral_eq_zero
2221     {Omega : Type u} {K : Nat}
2222     [mOmega : MeasurableSpace Omega] [StandardBorelSpace Omega]
2223     (mu : Measure Omega) [IsFiniteMeasure mu]
2224     (F : Filtration Nat mOmega)
2225     (model : FiniteBanditModel K)
2226     (reward : Omega -> RewardTrace Rat)
2227     (b : Fin K) (i : Nat)
2228     (h_integrable :
2229       Integrable
2230         (fun omega : Omega =>
2231           (((reward omega (i + 1) - model.mean b : Rat) : Real))) mu)
2232     (h_kernel_zero :
2233       Filter.Eventually
2234         (fun omega : Omega =>
2235           integral
2236             (ProbabilityTheory.condExpKernel
2237               (Ω := Omega) (mΩ := mOmega) mu (F i) omega)
2238             (fun y : Omega =>
2239               (((reward y (i + 1) - model.mean b : Rat) : Real))) = 0)
2240         (ae (mu.trim (F.le i)))) :
2241     Filter.EventuallyEq (ae mu)
2242       (@condExp Omega Real (F i) mOmega _ _ _ mu
2243         (fun omega : Omega =>
2244           (((reward omega (i + 1) - model.mean b : Rat) : Real))))
2245       (fun _omega : Omega => (0 : Real)) := by
2246   exact
2247     condExp_eq_zero_of_condExpKernel_integral_eq_zero
2248       (mu := mu)
2249       (mcond := F i)
2250       (hm := F.le i)
2251       (X := fun omega : Omega =>
2252         (((reward omega (i + 1) - model.mean b : Rat) : Real)))
2253       h_integrable
2254       h_kernel_zero

2256 /--
2257 Consumer bridge from a trajectory-law-shaped conditional-kernel identification
2258 to ordinary conditional mean zero.
2259 
2260 The hypothesis `h_kernel_eq` is intentionally explicit: it is the future
2261 `condExpKernel`/history-step reward-law identification, already reduced to the
2262 centered integral shape needed here.  The theorem then uses the compiled
2263 `RewardKernel.historyStepKernelFamily_centeredReward_integral_eq_zero` leaf and
2264 the generic `condExpKernel` bridge above.

2266 theorem condExp_eq_zero_of_condExpKernel_integral_eq_historyStepKernel_centeredReward
2267     {Omega : Type u} {Context : Type v} {State : Type w} {Action : Type x}
2268     [mOmega : MeasurableSpace Omega] [StandardBorelSpace Omega]
2269     [MeasurableSpace Context] [MeasurableSpace State]
2270     [MeasurableSpace Action]
2271     (mu : Measure Omega) [IsFiniteMeasure mu]
2272     (mcond : MeasurableSpace Omega) (hm : mcond <= mOmega)
2273     (rewardKernel : RewardKernel.MarkovRewardKernel (Context × Action) Rat)
2274     (policy : Nat -> Policy.MeasurablePolicy State Action)
2275     (context : (n : Nat) -> ((i : Finset.Iic n) -> Rat) -> Context)
2276     (state : (n : Nat) -> ((i : Finset.Iic n) -> Rat) -> State)
2277     (hcontext : forall n : Nat, Measurable (context n))
2278     (hstate : forall n : Nat, Measurable (state n))
2279     (mean : Context -> Action -> Rat)
2280     (varianceProxy : Context -> Action -> NNReal)
2281     (law :
2282       RewardKernel.CenteredRewardKernelLaw rewardKernel mean varianceProxy)
2283     (n : Nat)
2284     (history : Omega -> ((i : Finset.Iic n) -> Rat))
2285     (X : Omega -> Real)
2286     (h_integrable : Integrable X mu)
2287     (h_kernel_eq :
2288       Filter.Eventually
2289         (fun omega : Omega =>
2290           integral
2291             (ProbabilityTheory.condExpKernel
2292               (Ω := Omega) (mΩ := mOmega) mu mcond omega)
2293             X =
2294           integral
2295             (RewardKernel.historyStepKernelFamily rewardKernel policy context
2296               state hcontext hstate n (history omega))
2297             (fun reward : Rat =>
2298               (((reward -
2299                 mean (context n (history omega))
2300                   ((policy n).action (state n (history omega))) :
2301                     Rat) : Real))))
2302         (ae (mu.trim hm))) :
2303     Filter.EventuallyEq (ae mu)
2304       (@condExp Omega Real mcond mOmega _ _ _ mu X)
2305       (fun _omega : Omega => (0 : Real)) := by
2306   refine
2307     @condExp_eq_zero_of_condExpKernel_integral_eq_zero
2308       Omega mOmega inferInstance mu inferInstance mcond hm X h_integrable ?_
2309   filter_upwards [h_kernel_eq] with omega h_eq
2310   rw [h_eq]
2311   exact
2312     RewardKernel.historyStepKernelFamily_centeredReward_integral_eq_zero
2313       rewardKernel policy context state hcontext hstate mean varianceProxy law
2314       n (history omega)

2403 /--
2404 Map-law consumer for the history-step conditional mean-zero route.
2405 
2406 Compared with the integral-equality consumer above, this theorem assumes a
2407 more structural reward-law identification: trim-a.e., the conditional kernel
2408 pushed forward by the next-reward coordinate is the corresponding
2409 `historyStepKernelFamily` reward law.  The separate `h_kernel_X_eq` hypothesis
2410 records the usual "past is frozen under conditioning" obligation needed to
2411 replace the actual target variable by the centered function with the outer
2412 history fixed at `omega`.
2413 
2414 This still does not construct the `partialTraj`/`condExpKernel` identity; it
2415 only consumes a map-level version of that identity.
2416 -/
2417 theorem condExp_eq_zero_of_condExpKernel_map_eq_historyStepKernel_centeredReward
2418     {Omega : Type u} {Context : Type v} {State : Type w} {Action : Type x}

2420     [MeasurableSpace Context] [MeasurableSpace State]

2422     (mu : Measure Omega) [IsFiniteMeasure mu]
2423     (mcond : MeasurableSpace Omega) (hm : mcond <= mOmega)
2424     (rewardKernel : RewardKernel.MarkovRewardKernel (Context × Action) Rat)
2425     (policy : Nat -> Policy.MeasurablePolicy State Action)
2426     (context : (n : Nat) -> ((i : Finset.Iic n) -> Rat) -> Context)
2427     (state : (n : Nat) -> ((i : Finset.Iic n) -> Rat) -> State)
2428     (hcontext : forall n : Nat, Measurable (context n))
2429     (hstate : forall n : Nat, Measurable (state n))
2430     (mean : Context -> Action -> Rat)
2431     (varianceProxy : Context -> Action -> NNReal)
2432     (law :
2433       RewardKernel.CenteredRewardKernelLaw rewardKernel mean varianceProxy)
2434     (n : Nat)
2435     (history : Omega -> ((i : Finset.Iic n) -> Rat))
2436     (nextReward : Omega -> Rat)
2437     (X : Omega -> Real)
2438     (h_nextReward : @Measurable Omega Rat mOmega inferInstance nextReward)
2439     (h_integrable : Integrable X mu)
2440     (h_kernel_map_eq :
2441       Filter.Eventually
2442         (fun omega : Omega =>
2443           @Measure.map Omega Rat mOmega inferInstance nextReward
2444             (ProbabilityTheory.condExpKernel
2445               (Ω := Omega) (mΩ := mOmega) mu mcond omega) =
2446           RewardKernel.historyStepKernelFamily rewardKernel policy context state
2447             hcontext hstate n (history omega))
2448         (ae (mu.trim hm)))
2449     (h_kernel_X_eq :
2450       Filter.Eventually
2451         (fun omega : Omega =>
2452           X =ᵐ[
2453             ProbabilityTheory.condExpKernel
2454               (Ω := Omega) (mΩ := mOmega) mu mcond omega]
2455             (fun y : Omega =>
2456               (((nextReward y -
2457                 mean (context n (history omega))
2458                   ((policy n).action (state n (history omega))) :
2459                     Rat) : Real))))
2460         (ae (mu.trim hm))) :
2461     Filter.EventuallyEq (ae mu)
2462       (@condExp Omega Real mcond mOmega _ _ _ mu X)
2463       (fun _omega : Omega => (0 : Real)) := by
2464   refine
2465     @condExp_eq_zero_of_condExpKernel_integral_eq_zero
2466       Omega mOmega inferInstance mu inferInstance mcond hm X h_integrable ?_
2467   filter_upwards [h_kernel_map_eq, h_kernel_X_eq] with omega h_map h_X
2468   have h_centered_meas :
2469       Measurable
2470         (fun reward : Rat =>
2471           (((reward -
2472             mean (context n (history omega))
2473               ((policy n).action (state n (history omega))) : Rat) :
2474                 Real))) := by
2475     exact
2476       measurable_of_countable
2477         (fun reward : Rat =>
2478           (((reward -
2479             mean (context n (history omega))
2480               ((policy n).action (state n (history omega))) : Rat) :
2481                 Real)))

2483     integral
2484         (ProbabilityTheory.condExpKernel
2485           (Ω := Omega) (mΩ := mOmega) mu mcond omega)
2486         X
2487         =
2488       integral
2489         (ProbabilityTheory.condExpKernel
2490           (Ω := Omega) (mΩ := mOmega) mu mcond omega)
2491         (fun y : Omega =>
2492           (((nextReward y -
2493             mean (context n (history omega))
2494               ((policy n).action (state n (history omega))) : Rat) :
2495                 Real))) := by
2496           exact integral_congr_ae h_X
2497     _ =
2498       integral
2499         (@Measure.map Omega Rat mOmega inferInstance nextReward
2500           (ProbabilityTheory.condExpKernel
2501             (Ω := Omega) (mΩ := mOmega) mu mcond omega))
2502         (fun reward : Rat =>
2503           (((reward -
2504             mean (context n (history omega))
2505               ((policy n).action (state n (history omega))) : Rat) :
2506                 Real))) := by
2507           exact
2508             (integral_map
2509               h_nextReward.aemeasurable
2510               h_centered_meas.aestronglyMeasurable).symm
2511     _ =
2512       integral
2513         (RewardKernel.historyStepKernelFamily rewardKernel policy context state
2514           hcontext hstate n (history omega))
2515         (fun reward : Rat =>
2516           (((reward -
2517             mean (context n (history omega))
2518               ((policy n).action (state n (history omega))) : Rat) :
2519                 Real))) := by
2520           rw [h_map]
2521     _ = 0 := by

2527 /--
2528 Map-law consumer for the history-step conditional sub-Gaussian route.
2529 
2530 This is the MGF analogue of
2531 `condExp_eq_zero_of_condExpKernel_map_eq_historyStepKernel_centeredReward`.
2532 It keeps two contracts explicit: `h_kernel_X_eq` freezes the history-dependent
2533 centering under the conditional kernel, and `h_variance_le` bounds the
2534 history-selected variance proxy by the deterministic proxy `c` required by
2535 Mathlib's `HasCondSubgaussianMGF`.  Exponential integrability is generated by
2536 the generic integrated target-law transfer above.
2537 -/
2538 theorem hasCondSubgaussianMGF_of_condExpKernel_map_eq_historyStepKernel_centeredReward
2539     {Omega : Type u} {Context : Type v} {State : Type w} {Action : Type x}
2540     [mOmega : MeasurableSpace Omega] [StandardBorelSpace Omega]
2541     [MeasurableSpace Context] [MeasurableSpace State]
2542     [MeasurableSpace Action]
2543     (mu : Measure Omega) [IsFiniteMeasure mu]
2544     (mcond : MeasurableSpace Omega) (hm : mcond <= mOmega)
2545     (rewardKernel : RewardKernel.MarkovRewardKernel (Prod Context Action) Rat)
2546     (policy : Nat -> Policy.MeasurablePolicy State Action)
2547     (context : (n : Nat) -> ((i : Finset.Iic n) -> Rat) -> Context)
2548     (state : (n : Nat) -> ((i : Finset.Iic n) -> Rat) -> State)
2549     (hcontext : forall n : Nat, Measurable (context n))
2550     (hstate : forall n : Nat, Measurable (state n))
2551     (mean : Context -> Action -> Rat)
2552     (varianceProxy : Context -> Action -> NNReal)
2553     (law :
2554       RewardKernel.CenteredRewardKernelLaw rewardKernel mean varianceProxy)
2555     (n : Nat)
2556     (history : Omega -> ((i : Finset.Iic n) -> Rat))
2557     (nextReward : Omega -> Rat)
2558     (X : Omega -> Real)
2559     (c : NNReal)
2560     (h_nextReward : @Measurable Omega Rat mOmega inferInstance nextReward)
2561     (hX : @Measurable Omega Real mOmega inferInstance X)
2562     (h_kernel_map_eq :
2563       Filter.Eventually
2564         (fun omega : Omega =>
2565           @Measure.map Omega Rat mOmega inferInstance nextReward
2566             (@ProbabilityTheory.condExpKernel Omega mOmega _ mu _ mcond
2567               omega) =
2568           RewardKernel.historyStepKernelFamily rewardKernel policy context
2569             state hcontext hstate n (history omega))
2570         (ae (mu.trim hm)))
2571     (h_kernel_X_eq :
2572       Filter.Eventually
2573         (fun omega : Omega =>
2574           Filter.EventuallyEq
2575             (ae (@ProbabilityTheory.condExpKernel Omega mOmega _ mu _ mcond
2576               omega))
2577             X
2578             (fun y : Omega =>
2579               (((nextReward y - mean (context n (history omega))
2580                 ((policy n).action (state n (history omega))) : Rat) : Real))))
2581         (ae (mu.trim hm)))
2582     (h_variance_le :
2583       Filter.Eventually
2584         (fun omega : Omega =>
2585           varianceProxy (context n (history omega))
2586             ((policy n).action (state n (history omega))) <= c)
2587         (ae (mu.trim hm))) :
2588     ProbabilityTheory.HasCondSubgaussianMGF mcond hm X c mu := by
2589   let target : Omega -> Measure Real :=
2590     fun omega : Omega =>
2591       @Measure.map Rat Real inferInstance inferInstance
2592         (fun reward : Rat =>
2593           (((reward - mean (context n (history omega))
2594             ((policy n).action (state n (history omega))) : Rat) : Real)))
2595         (RewardKernel.historyStepKernelFamily rewardKernel policy context
2596           state hcontext hstate n (history omega))
2597   have h_target_map_eq :
2598       Filter.Eventually
2599         (fun omega : Omega =>
2600           @Measure.map Omega Real mOmega inferInstance X
2601             (@ProbabilityTheory.condExpKernel Omega mOmega _ mu _ mcond
2602               omega) =
2603           target omega)
2604         (ae (mu.trim hm)) := by
2605     filter_upwards [h_kernel_map_eq, h_kernel_X_eq] with omega hmap hXeq
2606     let condKernel : @Measure Omega mOmega :=
2607       @ProbabilityTheory.condExpKernel Omega mOmega _ mu _ mcond omega
2608     let centeredReward : Rat -> Real :=
2609       fun reward : Rat =>
2610         (((reward - mean (context n (history omega))
2611           ((policy n).action (state n (history omega))) : Rat) : Real))
2612     have h_centered_meas : Measurable centeredReward := by
2613       exact measurable_of_countable centeredReward
2614     have h_X_map :
2615         @Measure.map Omega Real mOmega inferInstance X condKernel =
2616           @Measure.map Omega Real mOmega inferInstance
2617             (fun y : Omega => centeredReward (nextReward y)) condKernel := by
2618       exact Measure.map_congr hXeq
2619     have h_comp :
2620         @Measure.map Rat Real inferInstance inferInstance centeredReward
2621             (@Measure.map Omega Rat mOmega inferInstance nextReward
2622               condKernel) =
2623           @Measure.map Omega Real mOmega inferInstance
2624             (fun y : Omega => centeredReward (nextReward y)) condKernel := by
2625       rw [Measure.map_map h_centered_meas h_nextReward]
2626       rfl
2627     calc
2628       @Measure.map Omega Real mOmega inferInstance X
2629           (@ProbabilityTheory.condExpKernel Omega mOmega _ mu _ mcond
2630             omega)
2631           =
2632         @Measure.map Omega Real mOmega inferInstance X condKernel := by
2633           rfl
2634       _ =
2635         @Measure.map Omega Real mOmega inferInstance
2636           (fun y : Omega => centeredReward (nextReward y)) condKernel :=
2637         h_X_map
2638       _ =
2639         @Measure.map Rat Real inferInstance inferInstance centeredReward
2640           (@Measure.map Omega Rat mOmega inferInstance nextReward
2641             condKernel) :=
2642         h_comp.symm
2643       _ =
2644         @Measure.map Rat Real inferInstance inferInstance centeredReward
2645           (RewardKernel.historyStepKernelFamily rewardKernel policy context
2646             state hcontext hstate n (history omega)) := by
2647         rw [hmap]
2648       _ = target omega := by
2649         rfl
2650   have h_target_subG :
2651       Filter.Eventually
2652         (fun omega : Omega =>
2653           ProbabilityTheory.HasSubgaussianMGF (fun z : Real => z) c
2654             (target omega))
2655         (ae (mu.trim hm)) := by
2656     filter_upwards [h_variance_le] with omega hvar
2657     let centeredReward : Rat -> Real :=
2658       fun reward : Rat =>
2659         (((reward - mean (context n (history omega))
2660           ((policy n).action (state n (history omega))) : Rat) : Real))
2661     have h_centered_meas : Measurable centeredReward := by
2662       exact measurable_of_countable centeredReward
2663     have h_subG :
2664         ProbabilityTheory.HasSubgaussianMGF centeredReward
2665           (varianceProxy (context n (history omega))
2666             ((policy n).action (state n (history omega))))
2667           (RewardKernel.historyStepKernelFamily rewardKernel policy context
2668             state hcontext hstate n (history omega)) := by
2669       simpa [centeredReward] using
2670         RewardKernel.historyStepKernelFamily_centeredReward_hasSubgaussianMGF
2671           rewardKernel policy context state hcontext hstate mean
2672           varianceProxy law n (history omega)
2673     have h_id_subG :
2674         ProbabilityTheory.HasSubgaussianMGF (fun z : Real => z)
2675           (varianceProxy (context n (history omega))
2676             ((policy n).action (state n (history omega))))
2677           (@Measure.map Rat Real inferInstance inferInstance centeredReward
2678             (RewardKernel.historyStepKernelFamily rewardKernel policy context
2679               state hcontext hstate n (history omega))) := by
2680       exact
2681         (ProbabilityTheory.HasSubgaussianMGF.id_map_iff
2682           h_centered_meas.aemeasurable).2 h_subG
2683     simpa [target, centeredReward] using
2684       hasSubgaussianMGF_mono_varianceProxy hvar h_id_subG
2685   exact
2686     hasCondSubgaussianMGF_of_condExpKernel_map_eq
2687       (mOmega := mOmega)
2688       (mu := mu)
2689       (mcond := mcond)
2690       (hm := hm)
2691       (X := X)
2692       (c := c)
2693       hX
2694       target
2695       h_target_map_eq
2696       h_target_subG

2698 /--
2699 Succ-indexed selected-reward specialization of the conditional sub-Gaussian
2700 map-law consumer.
2701 
2702 This is the MGF analogue of
2703 `centeredReward_succ_condExp_eq_zero_of_historyStepKernelFamily_condExpKernel_map_eq`.
2704 It keeps measurability and deterministic variance domination explicit; the
2705 kernel-side sub-Gaussian law now also discharges exponential integrability via
2706 the integrated target-law transfer.
2707 -/
2708 theorem centeredReward_succ_hasCondSubgaussianMGF_of_historyStepKernelFamily_condExpKernel_map_eq
2709     {Omega : Type u} {Context : Type v} {State : Type w} {Action : Type x}
2710     [mOmega : MeasurableSpace Omega] [StandardBorelSpace Omega]
2711     [MeasurableSpace Context] [MeasurableSpace State]
2712     [MeasurableSpace Action]
2713     (mu : Measure Omega) [IsFiniteMeasure mu]
2714     (F : Filtration Nat mOmega)
2715     (rewardKernel : RewardKernel.MarkovRewardKernel (Prod Context Action) Rat)
2716     (policy : Nat -> Policy.MeasurablePolicy State Action)
2717     (context : (n : Nat) -> ((j : Finset.Iic n) -> Rat) -> Context)
2718     (state : (n : Nat) -> ((j : Finset.Iic n) -> Rat) -> State)
2719     (hcontext : forall n : Nat, Measurable (context n))
2720     (hstate : forall n : Nat, Measurable (state n))
2721     (mean : Context -> Action -> Rat)
2722     (varianceProxy : Context -> Action -> NNReal)
2723     (law :
2724       RewardKernel.CenteredRewardKernelLaw rewardKernel mean varianceProxy)
2725     (reward : Omega -> RewardTrace Rat)
2726     (i : Nat)
2727     (history : Omega -> ((j : Finset.Iic i) -> Rat))
2728     (c : NNReal)
2729     (h_reward :
2730       @Measurable Omega Rat mOmega inferInstance
2731         (fun omega : Omega => reward omega (i + 1)))
2732     (h_centered_meas :
2733       @Measurable Omega Real mOmega inferInstance
2734         (fun omega : Omega =>
2735           (((reward omega (i + 1) -
2736             mean (context i (history omega))
2737               ((policy i).action (state i (history omega))) : Rat) :
2738                 Real))))
2739     (h_kernel_map_eq :
2740       Filter.Eventually
2741         (fun omega : Omega =>
2742           @Measure.map Omega Rat mOmega inferInstance
2743             (fun y : Omega => reward y (i + 1))
2744             (ProbabilityTheory.condExpKernel
2745               (Ω := Omega) (mΩ := mOmega) mu (F i) omega) =
2746           RewardKernel.historyStepKernelFamily rewardKernel policy context state
2747             hcontext hstate i (history omega))
2748         (ae (mu.trim (F.le i))))
2749     (h_kernel_X_eq :
2750       Filter.Eventually
2751         (fun omega : Omega =>
2752           Filter.EventuallyEq
2753             (ae
2754               (ProbabilityTheory.condExpKernel
2755                 (Ω := Omega) (mΩ := mOmega) mu (F i) omega))
2756             (fun y : Omega =>
2757               (((reward y (i + 1) -
2758                 mean (context i (history y))
2759                   ((policy i).action (state i (history y))) : Rat) :
2760                     Real)))
2761             (fun y : Omega =>
2762               (((reward y (i + 1) -
2763                 mean (context i (history omega))
2764                   ((policy i).action (state i (history omega))) : Rat) :
2765                     Real))))
2766         (ae (mu.trim (F.le i))))
2767     (h_variance_le :
2768       Filter.Eventually
2769         (fun omega : Omega =>
2770           varianceProxy (context i (history omega))
2771             ((policy i).action (state i (history omega))) <= c)
2772         (ae (mu.trim (F.le i)))) :
2773     ProbabilityTheory.HasCondSubgaussianMGF (F i) (F.le i)
2774       (fun omega : Omega =>
2775         (((reward omega (i + 1) -
2776           mean (context i (history omega))
2777             ((policy i).action (state i (history omega))) : Rat) : Real)))
2778       c mu := by
2779   exact

2781       (mu := mu)

2783       (hm := F.le i)
2784       (rewardKernel := rewardKernel)
2785       (policy := policy)
2786       (context := context)
2787       (state := state)
2788       (hcontext := hcontext)
2789       (hstate := hstate)
2790       (mean := mean)
2791       (varianceProxy := varianceProxy)
2792       (law := law)
2793       (n := i)
2794       (history := history)
2795       (nextReward := fun omega : Omega => reward omega (i + 1))
2796       (X := fun omega : Omega =>
2797         (((reward omega (i + 1) -
2798           mean (context i (history omega))
2799             ((policy i).action (state i (history omega))) : Rat) :
2800               Real)))

2808 /--
2809 Event-level frozen-past canary for conditional-expectation kernels.
2810 
2811 If an event is measurable in the conditioning sigma-algebra, then trim-a.e. the
2812 conditional kernel assigns its real mass as the event indicator.  This is the
2813 0/1 event support fact used to freeze countable-valued past summaries below.
2814 -/
2815 theorem condExpKernel_event_real_eq_indicator_of_measurableSet
2816     {Omega : Type u}
2817     [mOmega : MeasurableSpace Omega] [StandardBorelSpace Omega]
2818     (mu : Measure Omega) [IsFiniteMeasure mu]
2819     (mcond : MeasurableSpace Omega) (hm : mcond <= mOmega)
2820     (s : Set Omega) (hs : @MeasurableSet Omega mcond s) :
2821     Filter.Eventually
2822       (fun omega : Omega =>
2823         (@ProbabilityTheory.condExpKernel Omega mOmega _ mu _ mcond omega).real s =
2824         Set.indicator s (fun _omega : Omega => (1 : Real)) omega)
2825       (ae (mu.trim hm)) := by
2826   let f : Omega -> Real :=
2827     Set.indicator s (fun _omega : Omega => (1 : Real))
2828   have hs_mOmega : @MeasurableSet Omega mOmega s := hm s hs
2829   have h_kernel :
2830       Filter.EventuallyEq (ae (mu.trim hm))
2831         (fun omega : Omega =>
2832           (@ProbabilityTheory.condExpKernel Omega mOmega _ mu _ mcond omega).real s)
2833         (@condExp Omega Real mcond mOmega _ _ _ mu f) := by
2834     simpa [f] using
2835       (@ProbabilityTheory.condExpKernel_ae_eq_trim_condExp
2836         Omega mcond mOmega _ mu _ hm s hs_mOmega)
2837   have hf_strong : StronglyMeasurable[mcond] f := by
2838     exact (Measurable.indicator measurable_const hs).stronglyMeasurable
2839   have hf_integrable : Integrable f mu := by
2840     exact (integrable_const (1 : Real)).indicator hs_mOmega
2841   have h_cond :
2842       @condExp Omega Real mcond mOmega _ _ _ mu f = f := by
2843     exact condExp_of_stronglyMeasurable hm hf_strong hf_integrable
2844   exact h_kernel.trans (Filter.EventuallyEq.of_eq h_cond)
2845 
2846 /--
2847 Countable-valued frozen-past theorem for conditional-expectation kernels.
2848 
2849 Any countable-valued random variable measurable in the conditioning
2850 sigma-algebra is trim-a.e. constant under the corresponding conditional
2851 kernel.  This packages the event-level 0/1 fact over all singleton fibers.
2852 -/
2853 theorem condExpKernel_ae_eq_const_of_countable_measurable
2854     {Omega : Type u} {A : Type v}
2855     [mOmega : MeasurableSpace Omega] [StandardBorelSpace Omega]
2856     [MeasurableSpace A] [MeasurableSingletonClass A] [Countable A]
2857     (mu : Measure Omega) [IsFiniteMeasure mu]
2858     (mcond : MeasurableSpace Omega) (hm : mcond <= mOmega)
2859     (Y : Omega -> A)
2860     (hY : @Measurable Omega A mcond inferInstance Y) :
2861     Filter.Eventually
2862       (fun omega : Omega =>
2863         Filter.EventuallyEq
2864           (ae (@ProbabilityTheory.condExpKernel Omega mOmega _ mu _ mcond omega))
2865           Y
2866           (fun _y : Omega => Y omega))
2867       (ae (mu.trim hm)) := by
2868   classical
2869   have h_fibers :
2870       Filter.Eventually
2871         (fun omega : Omega =>
2872           forall a : A,
2873             (@ProbabilityTheory.condExpKernel Omega mOmega _ mu _ mcond omega).real
2874                 (fun z : Omega => Y z = a) =
2875               Set.indicator (fun z : Omega => Y z = a)
2876                 (fun _z : Omega => (1 : Real)) omega)
2877         (ae (mu.trim hm)) := by
2878     rw [ae_all_iff]
2879     intro a
2880     have hs_fiber : @MeasurableSet Omega mcond (fun z : Omega => Y z = a) := by
2881       exact hY (MeasurableSet.singleton a)
2882     exact
2883       @condExpKernel_event_real_eq_indicator_of_measurableSet
2884         Omega mOmega inferInstance mu inferInstance mcond hm
2885         (fun z : Omega => Y z = a) hs_fiber
2886   filter_upwards [h_fibers] with omega homega
2887   let fiber : Set Omega := fun z : Omega => Y z = Y omega
2888   have hs_mcond : @MeasurableSet Omega mcond fiber := by
2889     exact hY (MeasurableSet.singleton (Y omega))
2890   have hs_mOmega : @MeasurableSet Omega mOmega fiber := hm fiber hs_mcond
2891   have h_indicator :
2892       Set.indicator (fun z : Omega => Y z = Y omega)
2893         (fun _z : Omega => (1 : Real)) omega = 1 := by
2894     rw [Set.indicator_of_mem]
2895     rfl
2896   have h_real_one :
2897       (@ProbabilityTheory.condExpKernel Omega mOmega _ mu _ mcond omega).real
2898         fiber = 1 := by
2899     have h := homega (Y omega)
2900     rw [h_indicator] at h
2901     simpa [fiber] using h
2902   have h_measure_one :
2903       (@ProbabilityTheory.condExpKernel Omega mOmega _ mu _ mcond omega)
2904         fiber = 1 := by
2905     exact (ENNReal.toReal_eq_one_iff _).mp (by
2906       simpa [Measure.real, measureReal_def] using h_real_one)
2907   haveI : IsProbabilityMeasure
2908       (@ProbabilityTheory.condExpKernel Omega mOmega _ mu _ mcond omega) := by
2909     infer_instance
2910   have h_ae_mem :
2911       Filter.Eventually
2912         (fun x : Omega => fiber x)
2913         (ae (@ProbabilityTheory.condExpKernel Omega mOmega _ mu _ mcond omega)) := by
2914     exact (mem_ae_iff_prob_eq_one hs_mOmega).2 h_measure_one
2915   change Filter.Eventually
2916     (fun z : Omega => Y z = Y omega)
2917     (ae (@ProbabilityTheory.condExpKernel Omega mOmega _ mu _ mcond omega))
2918   exact h_ae_mem
2919 
2920 /--
2921 Canonical `trajMeasure` full finite-prefix law in `condExpKernel.map` form.
2922 
2923 The earlier canonical extension-map theorem states the law for the deterministic
2924 extension of the frozen prefix by the random next pair.  This wrapper uses the
2925 standard `condExpKernel` frozen-prefix property for the conditioning map
2926 `Preorder.frestrictLe n`, then rewrites that extension map back to the full
2927 `Preorder.frestrictLe (n + 1)` prefix under the conditional kernel.
2928 -/
2929 theorem actionRewardPartialTrajectoryKernel_prefix_condExpKernel_map_trajMeasure
2930     {Context : Type x} {State : Type u} {Action : Type v}
2931     {Reward : Type w}
2932     [MeasurableSpace Context] [MeasurableSpace State]
2933     [MeasurableSpace Action] [MeasurableSpace Reward]
2934     [StandardBorelSpace (Prod Action Reward)]
2935     [StandardBorelSpace ((t : Nat) -> Prod Action Reward)]
2936     [Nonempty (Prod Action Reward)]
2937     [Nonempty ((t : Nat) -> Prod Action Reward)]
2938     [MeasurableSingletonClass (Prod Action Reward)]
2939     [Countable (Prod Action Reward)]
2940     (mu0 : Measure (Prod Action Reward))
2941     [MeasureTheory.IsProbabilityMeasure mu0]
2942     (rewardKernel : RewardKernel.MarkovRewardKernel (Prod Context Action) Reward)
2943     (policy : Nat -> Policy.MeasurablePolicy State Action)
2944     (context :
2945       (n : Nat) -> ((i : Finset.Iic n) -> Prod Action Reward) -> Context)
2946     (state :
2947       (n : Nat) -> ((i : Finset.Iic n) -> Prod Action Reward) -> State)
2948     (hcontext : forall n : Nat, Measurable (context n))
2949     (hstate : forall n : Nat, Measurable (state n))
2950     (n : Nat) :
2951     let stepKernel :=
2952       RewardKernel.actionRewardHistoryStepKernelFamily rewardKernel policy
2953         context state hcontext hstate
2954     let trajMeasure :=
2955       ProbabilityTheory.Kernel.trajMeasure
2956         (X := fun _ : Nat => Prod Action Reward) mu0 stepKernel
2957     Filter.Eventually
2958       (fun trajectory : (t : Nat) -> Prod Action Reward =>
2959         @Measure.map
2960           ((t : Nat) -> Prod Action Reward)
2961           ((i : Finset.Iic (n + 1)) -> Prod Action Reward)
2962           inferInstance inferInstance
2963           (Preorder.frestrictLe (n + 1))
2964           (@ProbabilityTheory.condExpKernel
2965             ((t : Nat) -> Prod Action Reward) inferInstance _ trajMeasure _
2966             ((inferInstance :
2967               MeasurableSpace
2968                 ((i : Finset.Iic n) -> Prod Action Reward)).comap
2969               (Preorder.frestrictLe n))
2970             trajectory) =
2971         RewardKernel.actionRewardPartialTrajectoryKernel rewardKernel policy
2972           context state hcontext hstate n (n + 1)
2973           (Preorder.frestrictLe n trajectory))
2974       (ae trajMeasure) := by
2975   let stepKernel :=
2976     RewardKernel.actionRewardHistoryStepKernelFamily rewardKernel policy
2977       context state hcontext hstate
2978   let trajMeasure :=
2979     ProbabilityTheory.Kernel.trajMeasure
2980       (X := fun _ : Nat => Prod Action Reward) mu0 stepKernel
2981   let prefixMap :
2982       ((t : Nat) -> Prod Action Reward) ->
2983         ((i : Finset.Iic n) -> Prod Action Reward) :=
2984     Preorder.frestrictLe n
2985   let fullPrefix :
2986       ((t : Nat) -> Prod Action Reward) ->
2987         ((i : Finset.Iic (n + 1)) -> Prod Action Reward) :=
2988     Preorder.frestrictLe (n + 1)
2989   have h_prefix_meas : Measurable prefixMap := by
2990     fun_prop
2991   have h_extend :=
2992     actionRewardPartialTrajectoryKernel_extend_condExpKernel_map_trajMeasure
2993       mu0 rewardKernel policy context state hcontext hstate n
2994   have h_frozen_trim :
2995       Filter.Eventually
2996         (fun trajectory : (t : Nat) -> Prod Action Reward =>
2997           Filter.EventuallyEq
2998             (ae
2999               (@ProbabilityTheory.condExpKernel
3000                 ((t : Nat) -> Prod Action Reward) inferInstance _
3001                 trajMeasure _
3002                 ((inferInstance :
3003                   MeasurableSpace
3004                     ((i : Finset.Iic n) -> Prod Action Reward)).comap
3005                   prefixMap)
3006                 trajectory))
3007             prefixMap
3008             (fun _y : (t : Nat) -> Prod Action Reward => prefixMap trajectory))
3009         (ae (trajMeasure.trim h_prefix_meas.comap_le)) := by
3010     exact
3011       condExpKernel_ae_eq_const_of_countable_measurable
3012         (mu := trajMeasure)
3013         (mcond :=
3014           ((inferInstance :
3015             MeasurableSpace
3016               ((i : Finset.Iic n) -> Prod Action Reward)).comap
3017             prefixMap))
3018         (hm := h_prefix_meas.comap_le)
3019         (Y := prefixMap)
3020         (comap_measurable prefixMap)
3021   have h_frozen :
3022       Filter.Eventually
3023         (fun trajectory : (t : Nat) -> Prod Action Reward =>
3024           Filter.EventuallyEq
3025             (ae
3026               (@ProbabilityTheory.condExpKernel
3027                 ((t : Nat) -> Prod Action Reward) inferInstance _
3028                 trajMeasure _
3029                 ((inferInstance :
3030                   MeasurableSpace
3031                     ((i : Finset.Iic n) -> Prod Action Reward)).comap
3032                   prefixMap)
3033                 trajectory))
3034             prefixMap
3035             (fun _y : (t : Nat) -> Prod Action Reward => prefixMap trajectory))
3036         (ae trajMeasure) :=
3037     ae_of_ae_trim h_prefix_meas.comap_le h_frozen_trim
3038   filter_upwards [h_extend, h_frozen] with trajectory h_extend_eq h_prefix_frozen
3039   let condKernel : Measure ((t : Nat) -> Prod Action Reward) :=
3040     @ProbabilityTheory.condExpKernel
3041       ((t : Nat) -> Prod Action Reward) inferInstance _ trajMeasure _
3042       ((inferInstance :
3043         MeasurableSpace ((i : Finset.Iic n) -> Prod Action Reward)).comap
3044         prefixMap)
3045       trajectory
3046   let extendFromFrozen :
3047       ((t : Nat) -> Prod Action Reward) ->
3048         ((i : Finset.Iic (n + 1)) -> Prod Action Reward) :=
3049     fun y =>
3050       History.extendPairHistorySucc
3051         (Preorder.frestrictLe n trajectory)
3052         (y (n + 1))
3053   have h_full_eq_extend :
3054       Filter.EventuallyEq (ae condKernel) fullPrefix extendFromFrozen := by
3055     filter_upwards [h_prefix_frozen] with y h_frozen_y
3056     funext j
3057     by_cases hj : j.1 <= n
3058     · have h_prefix_coord :=
3059         congrFun h_frozen_y ⟨j.1, Finset.mem_Iic.mpr hj⟩
3060       simpa [fullPrefix, prefixMap, extendFromFrozen,
3061         History.extendPairHistorySucc, hj] using h_prefix_coord
3062     · have hj_le : j.1 <= n + 1 := Finset.mem_Iic.mp j.2
3063       have h_succ_le : n + 1 <= j.1 :=
3064         Nat.succ_le_of_lt (lt_of_not_ge hj)
3065       have hj_eq : j.1 = n + 1 := le_antisymm hj_le h_succ_le
3066       simp [fullPrefix, extendFromFrozen, History.extendPairHistorySucc, hj_eq]
3067   have h_map_congr :
3068       @Measure.map
3069           ((t : Nat) -> Prod Action Reward)
3070           ((i : Finset.Iic (n + 1)) -> Prod Action Reward)
3071           inferInstance inferInstance fullPrefix condKernel =
3072         @Measure.map
3073           ((t : Nat) -> Prod Action Reward)
3074           ((i : Finset.Iic (n + 1)) -> Prod Action Reward)
3075           inferInstance inferInstance extendFromFrozen condKernel := by
3076     exact Measure.map_congr h_full_eq_extend
3077   calc
3078     @Measure.map
3079         ((t : Nat) -> Prod Action Reward)
3080         ((i : Finset.Iic (n + 1)) -> Prod Action Reward)
3081         inferInstance inferInstance
3082         (Preorder.frestrictLe (n + 1))
3083         (@ProbabilityTheory.condExpKernel
3084           ((t : Nat) -> Prod Action Reward) inferInstance _ trajMeasure _
3085           ((inferInstance :
3086             MeasurableSpace
3087               ((i : Finset.Iic n) -> Prod Action Reward)).comap
3088             (Preorder.frestrictLe n))
3089           trajectory)
3090         =
3091       @Measure.map
3092         ((t : Nat) -> Prod Action Reward)
3093         ((i : Finset.Iic (n + 1)) -> Prod Action Reward)
3094         inferInstance inferInstance extendFromFrozen condKernel := by
3095         simpa [condKernel, prefixMap, fullPrefix] using h_map_congr
3096     _ =
3097       RewardKernel.actionRewardPartialTrajectoryKernel rewardKernel policy
3098         context state hcontext hstate n (n + 1)
3099         (Preorder.frestrictLe n trajectory) := by
3100         simpa [condKernel, prefixMap, extendFromFrozen, stepKernel, trajMeasure]
3101           using h_extend_eq

3112 theorem actionRewardPartialTrajectoryKernel_finitePairHistoryOfTrace_condExpKernel_map_trajMeasure
3113     {Context : Type x} {State : Type u} {Action : Type v}
3114     {Reward : Type w}
3115     [MeasurableSpace Context] [MeasurableSpace State]
3116     [MeasurableSpace Action] [MeasurableSpace Reward]
3117     [StandardBorelSpace (Prod Action Reward)]
3118     [StandardBorelSpace ((t : Nat) -> Prod Action Reward)]
3119     [Nonempty (Prod Action Reward)]
3120     [Nonempty ((t : Nat) -> Prod Action Reward)]
3121     [MeasurableSingletonClass (Prod Action Reward)]
3122     [Countable (Prod Action Reward)]
3123     (mu0 : Measure (Prod Action Reward))
3124     [MeasureTheory.IsProbabilityMeasure mu0]
3125     (rewardKernel : RewardKernel.MarkovRewardKernel (Prod Context Action) Reward)
3126     (policy : Nat -> Policy.MeasurablePolicy State Action)
3127     (context :
3128       (n : Nat) -> ((i : Finset.Iic n) -> Prod Action Reward) -> Context)
3129     (state :
3130       (n : Nat) -> ((i : Finset.Iic n) -> Prod Action Reward) -> State)
3131     (hcontext : forall n : Nat, Measurable (context n))
3132     (hstate : forall n : Nat, Measurable (state n))
3133     (n : Nat) :
3134     let stepKernel :=
3135       RewardKernel.actionRewardHistoryStepKernelFamily rewardKernel policy
3136         context state hcontext hstate
3137     let trajMeasure :=
3138       ProbabilityTheory.Kernel.trajMeasure
3139         (X := fun _ : Nat => Prod Action Reward) mu0 stepKernel
3140     let prefixMap :
3141         ((t : Nat) -> Prod Action Reward) ->
3142           ((i : Finset.Iic n) -> Prod Action Reward) :=
3143       fun y =>
3144         History.finitePairHistoryOfTrace
3145           (fun t => (y t).1) (fun t => (y t).2) n
3146     let fullPrefix :
3147         ((t : Nat) -> Prod Action Reward) ->
3148           ((i : Finset.Iic (n + 1)) -> Prod Action Reward) :=
3149       fun y =>
3150         History.finitePairHistoryOfTrace
3151           (fun t => (y t).1) (fun t => (y t).2) (n + 1)
3152     Filter.Eventually
3153       (fun trajectory : (t : Nat) -> Prod Action Reward =>
3154         @Measure.map
3155           ((t : Nat) -> Prod Action Reward)
3156           ((i : Finset.Iic (n + 1)) -> Prod Action Reward)
3157           inferInstance inferInstance
3158           fullPrefix
3159           (@ProbabilityTheory.condExpKernel
3160             ((t : Nat) -> Prod Action Reward) inferInstance _ trajMeasure _
3161             ((inferInstance :
3162               MeasurableSpace
3163                 ((i : Finset.Iic n) -> Prod Action Reward)).comap
3164               prefixMap)
3165             trajectory) =
3166         RewardKernel.actionRewardPartialTrajectoryKernel rewardKernel policy
3167           context state hcontext hstate n (n + 1)
3168           (prefixMap trajectory))
3169       (ae trajMeasure) := by
3170   have h_prefixMap :
3171       (fun y : (t : Nat) -> Prod Action Reward =>
3172         History.finitePairHistoryOfTrace
3173           (fun t => (y t).1) (fun t => (y t).2) n) =
3174         (Preorder.frestrictLe n :
3175           ((t : Nat) -> Prod Action Reward) ->
3176             ((i : Finset.Iic n) -> Prod Action Reward)) := by
3177     funext y j
3178     simp
3179   have h_fullPrefix :
3180       (Preorder.frestrictLe (n + 1) :
3181           ((t : Nat) -> Prod Action Reward) ->
3182             ((i : Finset.Iic (n + 1)) -> Prod Action Reward)) =
3183         (fun y : (t : Nat) -> Prod Action Reward =>
3184           History.extendPairHistorySucc
3185             (History.finitePairHistoryOfTrace
3186               (fun t => (y t).1) (fun t => (y t).2) n)
3187             (y (n + 1))) := by
3188     funext y j
3189     by_cases hj : j.1 <= n
3190     · simp [History.extendPairHistorySucc, hj]
3191     · have hj_le : j.1 <= n + 1 := Finset.mem_Iic.mp j.2
3192       have h_succ_le : n + 1 <= j.1 :=
3193         Nat.succ_le_of_lt (lt_of_not_ge hj)
3194       have hj_eq : j.1 = n + 1 := le_antisymm hj_le h_succ_le
3195       simp [History.extendPairHistorySucc, hj_eq]
3196   simpa [h_prefixMap, h_fullPrefix] using
3197     actionRewardPartialTrajectoryKernel_prefix_condExpKernel_map_trajMeasure
3198       mu0 rewardKernel policy context state hcontext hstate n

3200 /--
3201 Action-freezing hookup for the next-pair split-law route.
3202

3204 kernel freezes it.  If that frozen action is also trim-a.e. the
3205 policy-selected action for the finite pair history, this supplies exactly the

3207 `actionRewardHistoryStepKernelFamily_pair_map_eq_of_action_ae_eq_policy_reward_map_eq`.
3208 It does not prove the predictability or policy-generation equality hypotheses.

3555 /--
3556 Actual-action pair-law marginalization for the reward-coordinate route.
3557 
3558 Future trajectory-law work may identify, under the conditional kernel, the
3559 pair `(actual next action, next reward)` as a fixed-action product of the
3560 selected reward law.  Mapping that pair law through `Prod.snd` gives the
3561 actual-action reward-coordinate map law consumed by the generated-action
3562 route below.
3563 -/
3564 theorem reward_condExpKernel_map_eq_selected_actual_action_of_pair_map_eq
3565     {Omega : Type u} {Context : Type v} {Action : Type x}
3566     [mOmega : MeasurableSpace Omega] [StandardBorelSpace Omega]
3567     [MeasurableSpace Context] [MeasurableSpace Action]
3568     (mu : Measure Omega) [IsFiniteMeasure mu]
3569     (F : Filtration Nat mOmega)
3570     (rewardKernel : RewardKernel.MarkovRewardKernel (Prod Context Action) Rat)
3571     (pairContext :
3572       (n : Nat) -> ((j : Finset.Iic n) -> Prod Action Rat) -> Context)
3573     (action : Omega -> ActionTrace Action)
3574     (reward : Omega -> RewardTrace Rat)
3575     (i : Nat)
3576     (pairHistory : Omega -> ((j : Finset.Iic i) -> Prod Action Rat))
3577     (h_reward_next :
3578       @Measurable Omega Rat mOmega inferInstance
3579         (fun omega : Omega => reward omega (i + 1)))
3580     (h_pair_map_eq_actual_action :
3581       Filter.Eventually
3582         (fun omega : Omega =>
3583           @Measure.map Omega (Prod Action Rat) mOmega inferInstance
3584             (fun y : Omega => (action omega (i + 1), reward y (i + 1)))
3585             (@ProbabilityTheory.condExpKernel Omega mOmega _ mu _ (F i)
3586               omega) =
3587           Measure.map
3588             (Prod.mk (action omega (i + 1)))
3589             (RewardKernel.selectedMeasure rewardKernel
3590               (pairContext i (pairHistory omega))
3591               (action omega (i + 1))))

3593     Filter.Eventually

3612     have h_action_const :

3619     rw [Measure.map_map measurable_snd h_actualPair_meas]

3623     fun_prop

3627         selectedReward := by

3629     simp [Function.comp_def, selectedReward]

3631     @Measure.map Omega Rat mOmega inferInstance
3632         (fun y : Omega => reward y (i + 1)) condKernel

3635         exact h_left.symm

3639         simpa [condKernel, selectedReward, actualPair] using
3640           congrArg (Measure.map Prod.snd) h_pair
3641     _ = selectedReward := h_right

4084 /--
4085 Coordinate-measurability hookup for finite reward histories.

4088 level `F i`, the whole `finiteRewardHistoryOfTrace` object is measurable and
4089 therefore frozen by the conditional-expectation kernel.  This is still only a
4090 measurability bridge; it does not identify the conditional kernel with a
4091 trajectory/history-step reward law.

4120         History.finiteRewardHistoryOfTrace (reward omega) i)
4121       ?_
4122   letI : MeasurableSpace Omega := F i
4123   exact measurable_pi_lambda _ (fun j : Finset.Iic i => hreward j)

4125 /--
4126 Generated-history-filtration specialization of finite reward-history freezing.
4127 
4128 For the shifted history filtration `historyFiltrationSucc`, all reward
4129 coordinates up to `i` are visible at level `i`, so the finite reward history
4130 prefix is frozen trim-a.e. under its conditional-expectation kernel.  This
4131 closes the concrete finite-history measurability side of the frozen-past route;
4132 the remaining missing leaf is the reward-law/trajectory-law identification.

4134 theorem finiteRewardHistory_condExpKernel_frozen_historyFiltrationSucc
4135     {Omega : Type u} {Action : Type v}

4143     (hreward : forall t : Nat,
4144       Measurable (fun omega : Omega => reward omega t))
4145     (i : Nat) :
4146     Filter.Eventually
4147       (fun omega : Omega =>
4148         Filter.EventuallyEq
4149           (ae
4150             (@ProbabilityTheory.condExpKernel Omega mOmega _ mu _
4151               ((History.historyFiltrationSucc action reward haction hreward) i)
4152               omega))
4153           (fun y : Omega =>
4154             History.finiteRewardHistoryOfTrace (reward y) i)

4156             History.finiteRewardHistoryOfTrace (reward omega) i))
4157       (ae
4158         (mu.trim
4159           ((History.historyFiltrationSucc action reward haction hreward).le

4313 /--
4314 Successor finite-pair trace decomposition under any frozen-prefix hypothesis.
4315 
4316 If the old finite pair prefix is a.e. frozen at `omega`, then the full
4317 `i + 1` prefix is a.e. the deterministic successor extension of that frozen
4318 prefix by the random next `(Action, Reward)` pair.
4319 -/
4320 theorem finitePairHistory_succ_ae_eq_extend_of_pairHistory_frozen
4321     {Omega : Type u} {Action : Type v}
4322     [mOmega : MeasurableSpace Omega]
4323     (nu : Measure Omega)
4324     (action : Omega -> ActionTrace Action)
4325     (reward : Omega -> RewardTrace Rat)
4326     (i : Nat) (omega : Omega)
4327     (h_pair_history_frozen :
4328       Filter.EventuallyEq (ae nu)
4329         (fun y : Omega =>
4330           History.finitePairHistoryOfTrace (action y) (reward y) i)
4331         (fun _y : Omega =>
4332           History.finitePairHistoryOfTrace (action omega) (reward omega) i)) :
4333     Filter.EventuallyEq (ae nu)
4334       (fun y : Omega =>
4335         History.finitePairHistoryOfTrace (action y) (reward y) (i + 1))
4336       (fun y : Omega =>
4337         History.extendPairHistorySucc
4338           (History.finitePairHistoryOfTrace (action omega) (reward omega) i)
4339           (action y (i + 1), reward y (i + 1))) := by
4340   filter_upwards [h_pair_history_frozen] with y h_frozen
4341   calc
4342     History.finitePairHistoryOfTrace (action y) (reward y) (i + 1) =
4343         History.extendPairHistorySucc
4344           (History.finitePairHistoryOfTrace (action y) (reward y) i)
4345           (action y (i + 1), reward y (i + 1)) := by
4346           rw [History.finitePairHistoryOfTrace_succ]
4347     _ =
4348         History.extendPairHistorySucc
4349           (History.finitePairHistoryOfTrace (action omega) (reward omega) i)
4350           (action y (i + 1), reward y (i + 1)) := by
4351           rw [h_frozen]
4352 
4353 /--
4354 Generated-history conditional-kernel successor decomposition for pair traces.
4355 
4356 This packages the previous frozen pair-prefix theorem into the concrete
4357 `History.historyFiltrationSucc` conditional kernel.  It is not a joint law
4358 identification; it only rewrites the random `i + 1` pair trace into a frozen
4359 prefix plus random next pair under the conditional kernel.
4360 -/
4361 theorem finitePairHistory_succ_condExpKernel_ae_eq_extend_historyFiltrationSucc
4362     {Omega : Type u} {Action : Type v}
4363     [mOmega : MeasurableSpace Omega] [StandardBorelSpace Omega]
4364     [MeasurableSpace Action] [MeasurableSingletonClass Action]
4365     [Countable Action]

4371     (hreward : forall t : Nat,
4372       Measurable (fun omega : Omega => reward omega t))
4373     (i : Nat) :
4374     Filter.Eventually
4375       (fun omega : Omega =>
4376         Filter.EventuallyEq
4377           (ae
4378             (@ProbabilityTheory.condExpKernel Omega mOmega _ mu _
4379               ((History.historyFiltrationSucc action reward haction hreward) i)
4380               omega))

4383           (fun y : Omega =>
4384             History.extendPairHistorySucc
4385               (History.finitePairHistoryOfTrace (action omega) (reward omega) i)
4386               (action y (i + 1), reward y (i + 1))))
4387       (ae
4388         (mu.trim
4389           ((History.historyFiltrationSucc action reward haction hreward).le
4390             i))) := by

4396       (haction := haction)
4397       (hreward := hreward)
4398       (i := i)
4399   filter_upwards [h_frozen] with omega h_pair_history_frozen
4400   exact
4401     finitePairHistory_succ_ae_eq_extend_of_pairHistory_frozen
4402       (nu :=
4403         @ProbabilityTheory.condExpKernel Omega mOmega _ mu _
4404           ((History.historyFiltrationSucc action reward haction hreward) i)
4405           omega)
4406       (action := action)
4407       (reward := reward)
4408       (i := i)
4409       (omega := omega)
4410       h_pair_history_frozen

4412 /--
4413 Pushforward form of the generated-history successor decomposition.

4416 lemma upgrades it via Mathlib's `Measure.map_congr`, so later route cards can
4417 state the remaining trajectory-law input against the deterministic
4418 `extendPairHistorySucc` map instead of the full `i + 1` trace restriction.

4420 theorem finitePairHistory_succ_condExpKernel_map_eq_extend_historyFiltrationSucc
4421     {Omega : Type u} {Action : Type v}

4424     [Countable Action]

4432     (i : Nat) :
4433     Filter.Eventually
4434       (fun omega : Omega =>

4438             History.finitePairHistoryOfTrace (action y) (reward y) (i + 1))

4451             omega))
4452       (ae
4453         (mu.trim
4454           ((History.historyFiltrationSucc action reward haction hreward).le
4455             i))) := by
4456   have h_ae :=

4458       (mu := mu)
4459       (action := action)

4467 /--
4468 Frozen-history bridge for the succ-indexed map-law route.
4469 
4470 If the finite reward history is already frozen under the conditional kernel,
4471 then the history-selected context/action mean in the centered next-reward
4472 variable is frozen as well.  This is the deterministic part of the
4473 `h_kernel_X_eq` side condition consumed by the map-law conditional-expectation
4474 bridge; it deliberately does not prove that the history itself is frozen.

4476 theorem centeredReward_succ_frozenPast_ae_of_history_frozen
4477     {Omega : Type u} {Context : Type v} {State : Type w} {Action : Type x}
4478     [mOmega : MeasurableSpace Omega] [StandardBorelSpace Omega]
4479     [MeasurableSpace State] [MeasurableSpace Action]
4480     (mu : Measure Omega) [IsFiniteMeasure mu]
4481     (F : Filtration Nat mOmega)
4482     (policy : Nat -> Policy.MeasurablePolicy State Action)
4483     (context : (n : Nat) -> ((j : Finset.Iic n) -> Rat) -> Context)
4484     (state : (n : Nat) -> ((j : Finset.Iic n) -> Rat) -> State)
4485     (mean : Context -> Action -> Rat)
4486     (reward : Omega -> RewardTrace Rat)
4487     (i : Nat)
4488     (history : Omega -> ((j : Finset.Iic i) -> Rat))
4489     (h_history_frozen :
4490       Filter.Eventually
4491         (fun omega : Omega =>
4492           history =ᵐ[
4493             ProbabilityTheory.condExpKernel
4494               (Ω := Omega) (mΩ := mOmega) mu (F i) omega]
4495             (fun _y : Omega => history omega))
4496         (ae (mu.trim (F.le i)))) :
4497     Filter.Eventually
4498       (fun omega : Omega =>
4499         (fun y : Omega =>
4500           (((reward y (i + 1) -
4501             mean (context i (history y))
4502               ((policy i).action (state i (history y))) : Rat) :
4503                 Real))) =ᵐ[
4504           ProbabilityTheory.condExpKernel
4505             (Ω := Omega) (mΩ := mOmega) mu (F i) omega]
4506         (fun y : Omega =>
4507           (((reward y (i + 1) -
4508             mean (context i (history omega))
4509               ((policy i).action (state i (history omega))) : Rat) :
4510                 Real))))
4511       (ae (mu.trim (F.le i))) := by
4512   filter_upwards [h_history_frozen] with omega hhistory
4513   filter_upwards [hhistory] with y hy
4514   simp [hy]

4516 /--
4517 Conditional sub-Gaussian map-law consumer with the frozen-past side condition
4518 discharged from prefix coordinate measurability.
4519 
4520 The remaining structural input is the reward-coordinate pushforward identity
4521 from `condExpKernel` to the history-step reward kernel.  Ambient measurability
4522 of the centered reward and the deterministic variance-proxy upper bound remain
4523 explicit regularity contracts; exponential integrability is derived.
4524 -/
4525 theorem centeredReward_succ_hasCondSubgaussianMGF_of_historyStepKernelFamily_condExpKernel_map_eq_of_coordinate_measurable
4526     {Omega : Type u} {Context : Type v} {State : Type w} {Action : Type x}
4527     [mOmega : MeasurableSpace Omega] [StandardBorelSpace Omega]
4528     [MeasurableSpace Context] [MeasurableSpace State]
4529     [MeasurableSpace Action]
4530     (mu : Measure Omega) [IsFiniteMeasure mu]
4531     (F : Filtration Nat mOmega)
4532     (rewardKernel : RewardKernel.MarkovRewardKernel (Prod Context Action) Rat)
4533     (policy : Nat -> Policy.MeasurablePolicy State Action)
4534     (context : (n : Nat) -> ((j : Finset.Iic n) -> Rat) -> Context)
4535     (state : (n : Nat) -> ((j : Finset.Iic n) -> Rat) -> State)
4536     (hcontext : forall n : Nat, Measurable (context n))
4537     (hstate : forall n : Nat, Measurable (state n))
4538     (mean : Context -> Action -> Rat)
4539     (varianceProxy : Context -> Action -> NNReal)
4540     (law :
4541       RewardKernel.CenteredRewardKernelLaw rewardKernel mean varianceProxy)
4542     (reward : Omega -> RewardTrace Rat)
4543     (i : Nat)
4544     (c : NNReal)
4545     (h_reward :
4546       @Measurable Omega Rat mOmega inferInstance
4547         (fun omega : Omega => reward omega (i + 1)))
4548     (h_prefix_meas :
4549       forall j : Finset.Iic i,
4550         @Measurable Omega Rat (F i) inferInstance
4551           (fun omega : Omega => reward omega j.1))
4552     (h_centered_meas :
4553       @Measurable Omega Real mOmega inferInstance
4554         (fun omega : Omega =>
4555           (((reward omega (i + 1) -
4556             mean
4557               (context i
4558                 (History.finiteRewardHistoryOfTrace (reward omega) i))
4559               ((policy i).action
4560                 (state i
4561                   (History.finiteRewardHistoryOfTrace (reward omega) i))) :
4562                 Rat) : Real))))
4563     (h_kernel_map_eq :
4564       Filter.Eventually
4565         (fun omega : Omega =>
4566           @Measure.map Omega Rat mOmega inferInstance
4567             (fun y : Omega => reward y (i + 1))
4568             (@ProbabilityTheory.condExpKernel Omega mOmega _ mu _ (F i)
4569               omega) =
4570           RewardKernel.historyStepKernelFamily rewardKernel policy context state
4571             hcontext hstate i
4572             (History.finiteRewardHistoryOfTrace (reward omega) i))
4573         (ae (mu.trim (F.le i))))
4574     (h_variance_le :
4575       Filter.Eventually
4576         (fun omega : Omega =>
4577           varianceProxy
4578               (context i
4579                 (History.finiteRewardHistoryOfTrace (reward omega) i))
4580               ((policy i).action
4581                 (state i
4582                   (History.finiteRewardHistoryOfTrace (reward omega) i))) <=
4583             c)
4584         (ae (mu.trim (F.le i)))) :
4585     ProbabilityTheory.HasCondSubgaussianMGF (F i) (F.le i)
4586       (fun omega : Omega =>
4587         (((reward omega (i + 1) -
4588           mean
4589             (context i
4590               (History.finiteRewardHistoryOfTrace (reward omega) i))
4591             ((policy i).action
4592               (state i
4593                 (History.finiteRewardHistoryOfTrace (reward omega) i))) :
4594             Rat) : Real)))
4595       c mu := by
4596   let history : Omega -> ((j : Finset.Iic i) -> Rat) :=
4597     fun omega : Omega => History.finiteRewardHistoryOfTrace (reward omega) i
4598   have h_history_frozen :
4599       Filter.Eventually
4600         (fun omega : Omega =>
4601           Filter.EventuallyEq
4602             (ae
4603               (@ProbabilityTheory.condExpKernel Omega mOmega _ mu _ (F i)
4604                 omega))
4605             history
4606             (fun _y : Omega => history omega))
4607         (ae (mu.trim (F.le i))) := by
4608     simpa [history] using
4609       finiteRewardHistory_condExpKernel_frozen_of_coordinate_measurable
4610         (mu := mu)
4611         (F := F)
4612         (reward := reward)
4613         (i := i)
4614         h_prefix_meas
4615   have h_kernel_X_eq :
4616       Filter.Eventually
4617         (fun omega : Omega =>
4618           Filter.EventuallyEq
4619             (ae
4620               (@ProbabilityTheory.condExpKernel Omega mOmega _ mu _ (F i)
4621                 omega))
4622             (fun y : Omega =>
4623               (((reward y (i + 1) -
4624                 mean (context i (history y))
4625                   ((policy i).action (state i (history y))) : Rat) :
4626                     Real)))
4627             (fun y : Omega =>
4628               (((reward y (i + 1) -
4629                 mean (context i (history omega))
4630                   ((policy i).action (state i (history omega))) : Rat) :
4631                     Real))))
4632         (ae (mu.trim (F.le i))) := by
4633     exact
4634       centeredReward_succ_frozenPast_ae_of_history_frozen
4635         (mu := mu)
4636         (F := F)
4637         (policy := policy)
4638         (context := context)
4639         (state := state)
4640         (mean := mean)
4641         (reward := reward)
4642         (i := i)
4643         (history := history)
4644         h_history_frozen
4645   exact
4646     centeredReward_succ_hasCondSubgaussianMGF_of_historyStepKernelFamily_condExpKernel_map_eq
4647       (mu := mu)
4648       (F := F)
4649       (rewardKernel := rewardKernel)
4650       (policy := policy)
4651       (context := context)
4652       (state := state)
4653       (hcontext := hcontext)
4654       (hstate := hstate)
4655       (mean := mean)
4656       (varianceProxy := varianceProxy)
4657       (law := law)
4658       (reward := reward)
4659       (i := i)
4660       (history := history)
4661       (c := c)
4662       h_reward
4663       (by simpa [history] using h_centered_meas)
4664       (by simpa [history] using h_kernel_map_eq)
4665       h_kernel_X_eq
4666       (by simpa [history] using h_variance_le)
4667 
4668 /--
4669 Generated-history-filtration specialization of the conditional sub-Gaussian
4670 map-law consumer.
4671 
4672 At filtration level `History.historyFiltrationSucc ... i`, the reward prefix
4673 up to `i` is visible by construction, so the coordinate-measurable consumer
4674 applies directly.  The reward-coordinate pushforward identity and analytic
4675 measurability/variance contracts remain explicit, while exponential
4676 integrability is derived from the selected target laws.
4677 -/
4678 theorem centeredReward_succ_hasCondSubgaussianMGF_of_historyStepKernelFamily_condExpKernel_map_eq_historyFiltrationSucc

5116 /--
5117 Pair-law route into the map-law conditional mean-zero consumer.
5118 
5119 If the conditional kernel has the correct next `(Action × Reward)` law, then
5120 mapping both sides through `Prod.snd` gives the reward-coordinate law consumed
5121 by the map-law conditional-expectation bridge.  This theorem still assumes the
5122 pair-law identity; it only packages the marginalization step through
5123 `RewardKernel.actionRewardHistoryStepKernelFamily_reward_map`.

5125 theorem centeredReward_succ_condExp_eq_zero_of_actionRewardHistoryStepKernelFamily_pair_map_eq_of_coordinate_measurable

5157         (fun omega : Omega => reward omega (i + 1)))

5196             mean
5197               (context i

5199               ((policy i).action
5200                 (state i

5202                 Rat) : Real))))

5207     h_action_next.prod h_reward_next
5208   have h_kernel_map_eq :
5209       Filter.Eventually
5210         (fun omega : Omega =>
5211           @Measure.map Omega Rat mOmega inferInstance

5213             (@ProbabilityTheory.condExpKernel Omega mOmega _ mu _ (F i)
5214               omega) =
5215           RewardKernel.historyStepKernelFamily rewardKernel policy context state

5223       fun y : Omega => (action y (i + 1), reward y (i + 1))
5224     have h_map_map :
5225         Measure.map Prod.snd (Measure.map nextPair condKernel) =
5226           Measure.map (fun y : Omega => reward y (i + 1)) condKernel := by
5227       rw [Measure.map_map measurable_snd h_pair_next_meas]
5228       rfl
5229     calc
5230       @Measure.map Omega Rat mOmega inferInstance
5231           (fun y : Omega => reward y (i + 1))
5232           (@ProbabilityTheory.condExpKernel Omega mOmega _ mu _ (F i) omega)
5233           =
5234         Measure.map Prod.snd
5235           (@Measure.map Omega (Prod Action Rat) mOmega inferInstance
5236             (fun y : Omega => (action y (i + 1), reward y (i + 1)))
5237             (@ProbabilityTheory.condExpKernel Omega mOmega _ mu _ (F i)
5238               omega)) := by
5239           simpa [condKernel, nextPair] using h_map_map.symm
5240       _ =
5241         Measure.map Prod.snd
5242           (RewardKernel.actionRewardHistoryStepKernelFamily rewardKernel policy
5243             pairContext pairState hpairContext hpairState i
5244             (pairHistory omega)) := by
5245           rw [h_pair]
5246       _ =
5247         RewardKernel.selectedMeasure rewardKernel
5248           (pairContext i (pairHistory omega))
5249           ((policy i).action (pairState i (pairHistory omega))) := by
5250           exact
5251             RewardKernel.actionRewardHistoryStepKernelFamily_reward_map
5252               rewardKernel policy pairContext pairState hpairContext hpairState
5253               i (pairHistory omega)
5254       _ =
5255         RewardKernel.historyStepKernelFamily rewardKernel policy context state
5256           hcontext hstate i
5257           (History.finiteRewardHistoryOfTrace (reward omega) i) := by
5258           rw [h_pair_context_eq omega, h_pair_state_eq omega]
5259           rfl
5260   exact
5261     centeredReward_succ_condExp_eq_zero_of_historyStepKernelFamily_condExpKernel_map_eq_of_coordinate_measurable
5262       (mu := mu)
5263       (F := F)
5264       (rewardKernel := rewardKernel)
5265       (policy := policy)
5266       (context := context)
5267       (state := state)
5268       (hcontext := hcontext)
5269       (hstate := hstate)
5270       (mean := mean)
5271       (varianceProxy := varianceProxy)
5272       (law := law)
5273       (reward := reward)
5274       (i := i)
5275       (h_reward := h_reward_next)
5276       h_prefix_meas
5277       h_integrable
5278       h_kernel_map_eq

5280 /--
5281 Generated-history-filtration specialization of the action/reward pair-law route.
5282 
5283 At `History.historyFiltrationSucc ... i`, the next action/reward coordinates
5284 are measurable in the ambient space and the reward prefix up to `i` is visible
5285 at filtration level `i`.  Thus the coordinate-measurable pair-map consumer
5286 applies directly.  The actual action/reward pair-law pushforward identity
5287 remains an explicit assumption.

5363                 (state i

5365                 Rat) : Real))))

5367   refine

5369       (mu := mu)

5739 /--
5740 Project a generated-history `partialTraj` trace law to the next-pair law.
5741 
5742 This names the reusable law-identification step that was previously only
5743 embedded inside the centered-reward consumer below.  If the conditional kernel
5744 of the full finite pair trace at `i + 1` agrees with the one-step action/reward
5745 `partialTraj` kernel, then mapping both sides to the successor coordinate gives
5746 the concrete next `(Action, Reward)` pushforward law consumed by the pair-map
5747 route.
5748 -/
5749 theorem actionRewardHistoryStepKernelFamily_pair_map_eq_of_actionRewardPartialTrajectoryKernel_map_eq_historyFiltrationSucc_finitePairHistoryOfTrace
5750     {Omega : Type u} {Context : Type v} {State : Type w} {Action : Type x}
5751     [mOmega : MeasurableSpace Omega] [StandardBorelSpace Omega]
5752     [MeasurableSpace Context] [MeasurableSpace State]
5753     [MeasurableSpace Action] [MeasurableSingletonClass Action]
5754     (mu : Measure Omega) [IsFiniteMeasure mu]
5755     (action : Omega -> ActionTrace Action)
5756     (rewardKernel : RewardKernel.MarkovRewardKernel (Prod Context Action) Rat)
5757     (policy : Nat -> Policy.MeasurablePolicy State Action)
5758     (context : (n : Nat) -> ((j : Finset.Iic n) -> Rat) -> Context)
5759     (state : (n : Nat) -> ((j : Finset.Iic n) -> Rat) -> State)
