5949 /--
5950 Project a history-step next-pair law to the actual-action reward law.
5951 
5952 This is the direct reward-coordinate adapter for a conditional kernel law
5953 already stated at the next `(Action, Reward)` level.  Mapping both sides
5954 through `Prod.snd` reduces the target to
5955 `RewardKernel.actionRewardHistoryStepKernelFamily_reward_map`; the only
5956 remaining rewrite is the trim-a.e. successor action equality.
5957 -/
5958 theorem reward_condExpKernel_map_eq_selected_actual_action_of_actionRewardHistoryStepKernelFamily_pair_map_eq
5959     {Omega : Type u} {Context : Type v} {State : Type w} {Action : Type x}
5960     [mOmega : MeasurableSpace Omega] [StandardBorelSpace Omega]
5961     [MeasurableSpace Context] [MeasurableSpace State]
5962     [MeasurableSpace Action]
5963     (mu : Measure Omega) [IsFiniteMeasure mu]
5964     (F : Filtration Nat mOmega)
5965     (rewardKernel : RewardKernel.MarkovRewardKernel (Prod Context Action) Rat)
5966     (policy : Nat -> Policy.MeasurablePolicy State Action)
5967     (pairContext :
5968       (n : Nat) -> ((j : Finset.Iic n) -> Prod Action Rat) -> Context)
5969     (pairState :
5970       (n : Nat) -> ((j : Finset.Iic n) -> Prod Action Rat) -> State)
5971     (hpairContext : forall n : Nat, Measurable (pairContext n))
5972     (hpairState : forall n : Nat, Measurable (pairState n))
5973     (action : Omega -> ActionTrace Action)
5974     (reward : Omega -> RewardTrace Rat)
5975     (haction : forall t : Nat,
5976       Measurable (fun omega : Omega => action omega t))
5977     (hreward : forall t : Nat,
5978       Measurable (fun omega : Omega => reward omega t))
5979     (i : Nat)
5980     (pairHistory : Omega -> ((j : Finset.Iic i) -> Prod Action Rat))
5981     (h_action_policy_eq :
5982       Filter.Eventually
5983         (fun omega : Omega =>
5984           action omega (i + 1) =
5985             (policy i).action (pairState i (pairHistory omega)))
5986         (ae (mu.trim (F.le i))))
5987     (h_kernel_pair_map_eq :
5988       Filter.Eventually
5989         (fun omega : Omega =>
5990           @Measure.map Omega (Prod Action Rat) mOmega inferInstance
5991             (fun y : Omega => (action y (i + 1), reward y (i + 1)))
5992             (@ProbabilityTheory.condExpKernel Omega mOmega _ mu _ (F i)
5993               omega) =
5994           RewardKernel.actionRewardHistoryStepKernelFamily rewardKernel policy
5995             pairContext pairState hpairContext hpairState i
5996             (pairHistory omega))
5997         (ae (mu.trim (F.le i)))) :
5998     Filter.Eventually
5999       (fun omega : Omega =>
6000         @Measure.map Omega Rat mOmega inferInstance
6001           (fun y : Omega => reward y (i + 1))
6002           (@ProbabilityTheory.condExpKernel Omega mOmega _ mu _ (F i)
6003             omega) =
6004         RewardKernel.selectedMeasure rewardKernel
6005           (pairContext i (pairHistory omega))
6006           (action omega (i + 1)))
6007       (ae (mu.trim (F.le i))) := by
6008   filter_upwards [h_kernel_pair_map_eq, h_action_policy_eq] with omega
6009     h_pair h_action_eq
6010   let condKernel : Measure Omega :=
6011     @ProbabilityTheory.condExpKernel Omega mOmega _ mu _ (F i) omega
6012   let nextPair : Omega -> Prod Action Rat :=
6013     fun y : Omega => (action y (i + 1), reward y (i + 1))
6014   have h_nextPair_meas : Measurable nextPair := by
6015     exact (haction (i + 1)).prod (hreward (i + 1))
6016   have h_left :
6017       Measure.map Prod.snd (Measure.map nextPair condKernel) =
6018         @Measure.map Omega Rat mOmega inferInstance
6019           (fun y : Omega => reward y (i + 1)) condKernel := by
6020     rw [Measure.map_map measurable_snd h_nextPair_meas]
6021     rfl
6022   have h_step_reward :
6023       Measure.map Prod.snd
6024           (RewardKernel.actionRewardHistoryStepKernelFamily rewardKernel policy
6025             pairContext pairState hpairContext hpairState i
6026             (pairHistory omega)) =
6027         RewardKernel.selectedMeasure rewardKernel
6028           (pairContext i (pairHistory omega))
6029           ((policy i).action (pairState i (pairHistory omega))) := by
6030     exact
6031       RewardKernel.actionRewardHistoryStepKernelFamily_reward_map
6032         rewardKernel policy pairContext pairState hpairContext hpairState i
6033         (pairHistory omega)
6034   calc
6035     @Measure.map Omega Rat mOmega inferInstance
6036         (fun y : Omega => reward y (i + 1))
6037         (@ProbabilityTheory.condExpKernel Omega mOmega _ mu _ (F i) omega)
6038         =
6039       Measure.map Prod.snd (Measure.map nextPair condKernel) := by
6040         simpa [condKernel] using h_left.symm
6041     _ =
6042       Measure.map Prod.snd
6043         (RewardKernel.actionRewardHistoryStepKernelFamily rewardKernel policy
6044           pairContext pairState hpairContext hpairState i
6045           (pairHistory omega)) := by
6046         rw [h_pair]
6047     _ =
6048       RewardKernel.selectedMeasure rewardKernel
6049         (pairContext i (pairHistory omega))
6050         ((policy i).action (pairState i (pairHistory omega))) :=
6051         h_step_reward
6052     _ =
6053       RewardKernel.selectedMeasure rewardKernel
6054         (pairContext i (pairHistory omega))
6055         (action omega (i + 1)) := by
6056         rw [h_action_eq]

6292 /--
6293 Project a full finite-pair `partialTraj` law to the actual-action reward law.
6294 
6295 The full trace law first gives the next `(Action, Reward)` marginal by the
6296 compiled `partialTraj` next-coordinate adapter.  Mapping that marginal through
6297 `Prod.snd` and using `RewardKernel.actionRewardHistoryStepKernelFamily_reward_map`
6298 then gives the reward-coordinate law.  The final rewrite only needs an
6299 `i + 1` action equality against the policy-selected action.
6300 -/

6813           (fun n history =>
6814             context n (History.pairHistoryRewardProjection history))
6815           (fun n history =>
6816             state n (History.pairHistoryRewardProjection history))
6817           (fun n : Nat =>
6818             (hcontext n).comp
6819               (History.measurable_pairHistoryRewardProjection
6820                 (Action := Action) (Reward := Rat) n))
6821           (fun n : Nat =>
6822             (hstate n).comp
6823               (History.measurable_pairHistoryRewardProjection
6824                 (Action := Action) (Reward := Rat) n))
6825           i
6826           (History.finitePairHistoryOfTrace
6827             (action omega) (reward omega) i) := h_partial_step
6828   exact
6829     centeredReward_succ_condExp_eq_zero_of_actionRewardHistoryStepKernelFamily_pair_map_eq_historyFiltrationSucc_finitePairHistoryOfTrace
6830       (mu := mu)
6831       (action := action)
6832       (rewardKernel := rewardKernel)
6833       (policy := policy)
6834       (context := context)
6835       (state := state)
6836       (hcontext := hcontext)
6837       (hstate := hstate)
6838       (mean := mean)
6839       (varianceProxy := varianceProxy)
6840       (law := law)
6841       (reward := reward)
6842       (haction := haction)
6843       (hreward := hreward)
6844       (i := i)
6845       h_integrable
6846       h_kernel_pair_map_eq

6848 /--
6849 Build the full next `(Action, Reward)` conditional-kernel law from split
6850 action and reward laws.
6851 
6852 The action side is deterministic/predictable under the conditional kernel, and
6853 the reward side supplies the selected reward law.  Together they identify the
6854 next-pair pushforward with the local history-step action/reward kernel.
6855 -/
6856 theorem actionRewardHistoryStepKernelFamily_pair_map_eq_of_action_ae_eq_policy_reward_map_eq
6857     {Omega : Type u} {Context : Type v} {State : Type w} {Action : Type x}
6858     [mOmega : MeasurableSpace Omega] [StandardBorelSpace Omega]
6859     [MeasurableSpace Context] [MeasurableSpace State]
6860     [MeasurableSpace Action]
6861     (mu : Measure Omega) [IsFiniteMeasure mu]
6862     (F : Filtration Nat mOmega)
6863     (rewardKernel : RewardKernel.MarkovRewardKernel (Prod Context Action) Rat)
6864     (policy : Nat -> Policy.MeasurablePolicy State Action)
6865     (pairContext :
6866       (n : Nat) -> ((j : Finset.Iic n) -> Prod Action Rat) -> Context)
6867     (pairState :
6868       (n : Nat) -> ((j : Finset.Iic n) -> Prod Action Rat) -> State)
6869     (hpairContext : forall n : Nat, Measurable (pairContext n))
6870     (hpairState : forall n : Nat, Measurable (pairState n))
6871     (action : Omega -> ActionTrace Action)
6872     (reward : Omega -> RewardTrace Rat)
6873     (i : Nat)
6874     (pairHistory : Omega -> ((j : Finset.Iic i) -> Prod Action Rat))
6875     (h_action_next :
6876       @Measurable Omega Action mOmega inferInstance
6877         (fun omega : Omega => action omega (i + 1)))
6878     (h_reward_next :
6879       @Measurable Omega Rat mOmega inferInstance
6880         (fun omega : Omega => reward omega (i + 1)))
6881     (h_action_ae_eq_policy :
6882       Filter.Eventually
6883         (fun omega : Omega =>
6884           Filter.EventuallyEq
6885             (ae (@ProbabilityTheory.condExpKernel Omega mOmega _ mu _ (F i)
6886               omega))
6887             (fun y : Omega => action y (i + 1))
6888             (fun _y : Omega =>
6889               (policy i).action (pairState i (pairHistory omega))))
6890         (ae (mu.trim (F.le i))))
6891     (h_reward_map_eq :
6892       Filter.Eventually
6893         (fun omega : Omega =>
6894           @Measure.map Omega Rat mOmega inferInstance
6895             (fun y : Omega => reward y (i + 1))

6897               omega) =
6898           RewardKernel.selectedMeasure rewardKernel
6899             (pairContext i (pairHistory omega))
6900             ((policy i).action (pairState i (pairHistory omega))))
6901         (ae (mu.trim (F.le i)))) :

6905           (fun y : Omega => (action y (i + 1), reward y (i + 1)))

6907             omega) =
6908         RewardKernel.actionRewardHistoryStepKernelFamily rewardKernel policy
6909           pairContext pairState hpairContext hpairState i
6910           (pairHistory omega))
6911       (ae (mu.trim (F.le i))) := by
6912   have h_pair_next_meas :
6913       Measurable
6914         (fun y : Omega => (action y (i + 1), reward y (i + 1))) :=
6915     h_action_next.prod h_reward_next

6917     h_action h_reward

6919     @ProbabilityTheory.condExpKernel Omega mOmega _ mu _ (F i) omega
6920   let selectedAction : Action :=
6921     (policy i).action (pairState i (pairHistory omega))
6922   let rewardNext : Omega -> Rat :=
6923     fun y : Omega => reward y (i + 1)
6924   let nextPair : Omega -> Prod Action Rat :=
6925     fun y : Omega => (action y (i + 1), reward y (i + 1))
6926   let frozenPair : Omega -> Prod Action Rat :=
6927     fun y : Omega => (selectedAction, reward y (i + 1))
6928   have h_pair_ae :
6929       Filter.EventuallyEq (ae condKernel) nextPair frozenPair := by
6930     filter_upwards [h_action] with y hy
6931     simp [nextPair, frozenPair, selectedAction, hy]
6932   have h_next_to_frozen :
6933       Measure.map nextPair condKernel =
6934         Measure.map frozenPair condKernel := by
6935     exact Measure.map_congr h_pair_ae
6936   have h_prod_mk_meas : Measurable (Prod.mk selectedAction : Rat -> Prod Action Rat) := by
6937     fun_prop
6938   have h_frozen_map :
6939       Measure.map frozenPair condKernel =
6940         Measure.map (Prod.mk selectedAction)
6941           (Measure.map rewardNext condKernel) := by
6942     rw [Measure.map_map h_prod_mk_meas h_reward_next]
6943     rfl
6944   calc
6945     @Measure.map Omega (Prod Action Rat) mOmega inferInstance
6946         (fun y : Omega => (action y (i + 1), reward y (i + 1)))
6947         (@ProbabilityTheory.condExpKernel Omega mOmega _ mu _ (F i)
6948           omega)
6949         =
6950       Measure.map nextPair condKernel := by
6951         rfl
6952     _ = Measure.map frozenPair condKernel := h_next_to_frozen
6953     _ =
6954       Measure.map (Prod.mk selectedAction)
6955         (Measure.map rewardNext condKernel) := h_frozen_map
6956     _ =
6957       Measure.map (Prod.mk selectedAction)
6958         (RewardKernel.selectedMeasure rewardKernel
6959           (pairContext i (pairHistory omega))
6960           ((policy i).action (pairState i (pairHistory omega)))) := by
6961         rw [h_reward]
6962     _ =
6963       RewardKernel.actionRewardHistoryStepKernelFamily rewardKernel policy
6964         pairContext pairState hpairContext hpairState i
6965         (pairHistory omega) := by
6966         simpa [selectedAction] using

6968             rewardKernel policy pairContext pairState hpairContext hpairState
6969             i (pairHistory omega)).symm

6971 /--

6973 next-pair law.

6976 action side is supplied by `Policy.generatedActionTraceSucc`; the remaining
6977 reward assumption may be stated with the actual next action at the conditioning

7039             (action omega) (reward omega) i))

7043             i))) := by

8061 
8062 This is the next narrowing of the `partialTraj`/`condExpKernel` gap.  Instead
8063 of requiring a law identity for the full `i + 1` trace restriction, it only
8064 requires the conditional kernel pushed through the deterministic extension of
8065 the frozen old pair prefix by the random next `(Action, Reward)` pair to agree
